Custom effects feed the calculation ledger instead of becoming uncalculated notes.</div>

    <div class="rhe-box rhe-span12"><h3>Character Options &amp; Effects</h3><p class="rhe-mini">Each effect targets a named calculation key. Supported: + − × ÷ %, parentheses, min(), max(), floor(), ceil(), round(), abs().</p><div id="rheFormulas"></div><button type="button" id="rheAddFormula">Add Derived Formula</button></div>

Version 5.0 is dated 2023-06-27 and matches the fifth printing."},"what_savage_worlds_is":{"summary":"Savage Worlds is a genre-flexible tabletop roleplaying system designed for fast resolution, cinematic action, tactical play, and low Game Master bookkeeping.","supported_styles":["Narrative theater-of-the-mind play","Miniature-based tactical combat","Pulp adventure","Fantasy","Horror","Science fiction","Supers","Mystery and investigation","Military and large-scale action","Genre mixtures"],"core_design_features":["Attributes and skills are rated by die type rather than a numeric bonus.","Most ordinary Trait rolls use a Target Number of 4.","Important characters are Wild Cards and roll an extra d6 Wild Die.","Dice can Ace, meaning their maximum result is rerolled and added.","Every 4 points above the required total is a raise.","Players spend Bennies to reroll, Soak Wounds, alter initiative, and influence play.","Initiative normally uses a standard deck of playing cards with two Jokers.","Named heroes and major villains take multiple Wounds; most Extras are removed by one Wound.","Setting Rules deliberately tune the core engine for different genres and tones."]},"what_you_need":{"required":[{"item":"Core rules","details":"Savage Worlds Adventure Edition, preferably the version 5.0 PDF or fifth-printing book."},{"item":"Polyhedral dice","details":["d4","d6","d8","d10","d12"],"optional":["d20 for some tables","multiple d6s for damage"]},{"item":"Action Deck","details":"A standard 54-card deck: 52 playing cards plus two Jokers."},{"item":"Bennies","details":"Coins, poker chips, stones, or other visible tokens."},{"item":"Character sheet","details":"The official SWADE sheet or a compatible digital sheet."},{"item":"Game Master and players","details":"One person runs the world and rules; the other participants portray player characters."}],"optional":["A Savage Setting book","Miniatures, pawns, or tokens","Battle maps and rulers","Blast and cone templates","Status cards or markers","Power cards","A virtual tabletop","The free official Test Drive adventure"],"not_required":["The Savage Worlds Essentials boxed set","Custom dice","Miniatures","A grid"]},"roles_at_the_table":{"players":["Describe what their characters attempt.","Speak and make decisions for their characters.","Roll Traits when the outcome is uncertain and meaningful.","Track Bennies, Wounds, Fatigue, gear, powers, and temporary conditions.","Help establish character goals, relationships, and genre tone.","Respect the agreed safety tools and table boundaries."],"game_master":["Presents situations, locations, nonplayer characters, dangers, and consequences.","Decides when a roll is required and selects the relevant Trait.","Sets modifiers, opposition, and stakes.","Runs Extras, villains, hazards, and the Action Deck.","Awards Bennies for entertaining, thematic, clever, or heroic play.","Applies the core rules, setting rules, and current errata consistently.","Makes prompt rulings when an obscure question would otherwise stop play."],"shared_responsibilities":["Agree on the setting, campaign premise, tone, and allowable content.","Know which Setting Rules and optional subsystems are active.","Keep descriptions connected to the fiction rather than treating rolls as isolated buttons.","Correct honest rules mistakes without turning the session into an argument."]},"core_vocabulary":{"Trait":"An Attribute or Skill represented by a die type.","Attribute":"One of Agility, Smarts, Spirit, Strength, or Vigor.","Skill":"A learned capability such as Athletics, Fighting, Notice, Persuasion, Shooting, or Stealth.","Target_Number":"The total required for success. The usual unopposed Target Number is 4.","Raise":"Each full 4 points by which a successful result exceeds the required total.","Ace":"When a die rolls its maximum value, roll it again and add; it may Ace repeatedly.","Wild_Card":"A major character who rolls a Wild Die, has Bennies, and can normally suffer three Wounds.","Extra":"A supporting character who normally rolls no Wild Die and is Incapacitated by one Wound.","Wild_Die":"An extra d6 rolled with a Wild Card's Trait die; use the better result.","Benny":"A spendable token that gives a player or the Game Master limited control over luck and survival.","Action_Card":"A playing card that determines turn order in rounds.","Shaken":"A temporary rattled state that restricts actions until the character recovers.","Wound":"Serious harm that applies a cumulative penalty and can lead to Incapacitation.","Fatigue":"A separate track for exhaustion, exposure, and similar non-Wound harm.","Edge":"A beneficial character ability with requirements.","Hindrance":"A meaningful complication, flaw, obligation, or disadvantage.","Advance":"A character improvement earned during a campaign.","Rank":"A broad experience tier: Novice, Seasoned, Veteran, Heroic, or Legendary.","Setting_Rule":"A campaign-level rule that changes how the core engine behaves."},"the_basic_roll":{"procedure":["State what the character is trying to accomplish and how.","The Game Master decides whether a roll is necessary.","The Game Master selects the relevant Attribute or Skill and identifies modifiers or opposition.","Roll the Trait die.","If the character is a Wild Card, also roll a d6 Wild Die and use the better total.","Ace any die that rolls its maximum value by rerolling and adding.","Apply all modifiers to the chosen result.","Compare the total with the Target Number or opposing total.","Determine success, raises, failure, or Critical Failure.","Narrate the result and apply consequences."],"standard_target_number":4,"raises":{"rule":"Every full 4 points above the required total is one raise.","examples":[{"required":4,"total":4,"result":"Success"},{"required":4,"total":7,"result":"Success, no raise"},{"required":4,"total":8,"result":"Success with one raise"},{"required":4,"total":12,"result":"Success with two raises"}]},"wild_die":{"rule":"A Wild Card rolls the relevant Trait die and a d6 Wild Die, then uses the higher result. The dice are not added together.","modifiers":"Apply modifiers after selecting the better die total.","multiple_actions":"A Wild Card receives a Wild Die for each action, not one Wild Die for every individual attack die generated by Rate of Fire or another special rule."},"acing":{"rule":"Trait and damage dice are open-ended. When a die rolls its highest face, roll it again and add the new result. Repeat while maximum results continue.","example":{"die":"d6","rolls":[6,6,3],"total":15}},"critical_failure":{"wild_card":"A natural 1 on both the Trait die and Wild Die is a Critical Failure. It automatically fails, may create an additional complication, and cannot be rerolled with a Benny.","extra":"When it matters whether an Extra's natural 1 is critical, the Game Master may roll a d6; on a 1 it is a Critical Failure."},"unskilled_attempts":{"rule":"Roll d4-2, plus the Wild Die for a Wild Card, unless the skill cannot reasonably be attempted untrained.","warning":"The penalty applies to the selected total after choosing between the Trait die and Wild Die."},"opposed_rolls":{"procedure":["The acting character rolls first unless the specific rule says otherwise.","The opposing character rolls the resistance Trait.","The acting character must achieve at least a basic success and beat the opposing total.","Ties normally favor the defender or the status quo.","Every full 4 points over the opposing total is a raise."]},"when_not_to_roll":["The task is routine and there is no meaningful pressure.","Failure would not change the situation.","The character has enough time and resources that success is certain.","The roll would merely block essential information with no alternative route.","A different subsystem already resolves the situation."]},"probability_intuition":{"note":"These percentages show the chance to meet an unmodified Target Number of 4. They are included to help new players understand die steps; they are not normally needed during play.","ordinary_trait_roll":{"d4":"25.0%","d6":"50.0%","d8":"62.5%","d10":"70.0%","d12":"75.0%"},"wild_card_trait_plus_d6_wild_die":{"d4":"62.5%","d6":"75.0%","d8":"81.25%","d10":"85.0%","d12":"87.5%"},"unskilled_wild_card_d4_minus_2_plus_wild_die_minus_2":"Approximately 32.29%","lesson":"A die-step increase matters, but the Wild Die and Bennies make Wild Cards much more reliable than Extras without making success automatic."},"traits":{"attributes":{"Agility":"Coordination, dexterity, reflexes, and bodily control.","Smarts":"Reasoning, education, perception of patterns, and mental adaptability.","Spirit":"Willpower, courage, confidence, and inner resolve.","Strength":"Physical power and the basis of many melee damage rolls.","Vigor":"Endurance, health, resistance, Toughness, and Soak rolls."},"die_scale":["d4","d6","d8","d10","d12","d12+1 and higher for exceptional cases"],"skill_principles":["Skills are intentionally broad.","Each skill is linked to an Attribute for character-building cost.","A linked Attribute does not automatically add to the skill roll.","A setting may add, remove, rename, or restrict skills.","Arcane Backgrounds may provide specialized arcane skills."],"core_skills_starting_at_d4_in_standard_swade_creation":["Athletics","Common Knowledge","Notice","Persuasion","Stealth"],"common_core_skill_list":["Academics","Athletics","Battle","Boating","Common Knowledge","Driving","Electronics","Faith","Fighting","Focus","Gambling","Hacking","Healing","Intimidation","Language","Notice","Occult","Performance","Persuasion","Piloting","Psionics","Repair","Research","Riding","Science","Shooting","Spellcasting","Stealth","Survival","Taunt","Thievery","Weird Science"],"skill_list_warning":"Not every campaign uses every listed skill. A Critical Failure cannot be rerolled."},{"use":"Recover from Shaken","details":"Remove Shaken immediately, even outside the character's turn when allowed."},{"use":"Soak Wounds","details":"Immediately after one source causes Wounds, make a Vigor roll; each success and raise removes one incoming Wound."},{"use":"Draw a new Action Card","details":"After Action Cards and card-affecting Edges or Hindrances are resolved, discard the current card and draw another."},{"use":"Reroll damage","details":"Reroll the full damage roll, including a bonus damage die earned from an attack raise, and keep the better result."},{"use":"Regain Power Points","details":"A character with an Arcane Background may spend a Benny to regain 5 Power Points."},{"use":"Influence the story","details":"With Game Master approval, introduce a plausible clue, item, contact, or minor favorable detail. to perform complex tasks. function rollDie(sides){return Math.floor(Math.random()*sides)+1}
function rollExploding(sides){let total=0,parts=[],r;do{r=rollDie(sides);parts.push(r);total+=r}while(r===sides&&parts.length<50);return{total,parts,natural:parts[0]}}
function parseDice(expr,explode=false){const m=String(expr||"").trim().match(/^(\d*)d(\d+)(?:\s*([+-])\s*(\d+))?$/i);if(!m)return null;const count=clamp(Number(m[1]||1),1,100),sides=clamp(Number(m[2]),2,1000),mod=(m[3]==="-"?-1:1)*Number(m[4]||0);let rolls=[],total=mod;for(let i=0;i<count;i++){if(explode){const r=rollExploding(sides);rolls.push(r.parts.join("+"));total+=r.total}else{const r=rollDie(sides);rolls.push(String(r));total+=r}}return{total,rolls,mod,expression:expr}}
function downloadJson(obj,name){const blob=new Blob([JSON.stringify(obj,null,2)],{type:"application/json"}),url=URL.createObjectURL(blob),a=document.createElement("a");a.href=url;a.download=name;a.click();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),1500)}

function referenceSearch(){const term=($("referenceQuery")?.value||"").trim().toLowerCase(),box=$("referenceResults");if(term.length<2){box.textContent="Enter at least two characters.";return}const found=[];function walk(v,p,d){if(found.length>=60||d>10)return;if(v==null)return;if(["string","number","boolean"].includes(typeof v)){const s=String(v);if((p+" "+s).toLowerCase().includes(term))found.push(p+": "+s.slice(0,650));return}if(Array.isArray(v)){v.forEach((x,i)=>walk(x,p+"["+i+"]",d+1));return}Object.entries(v).forEach(([k,x])=>walk(x,p?p+"."+k:k,d+1))}walk(DB,"reference",0);box.textContent=found.length?found.join("\n\n"):"No match in this embedded reference."}
function imageFile(file,key,imgId){if(!file||!file.type.startsWith("image/"))return;const reader=new FileReader();reader.onload=()=>{state.profile[key]=reader.result;renderArtwork();scheduleSave()};reader.readAsDataURL(file)}
function renderArtwork(){[["portrait","portraitImg"],["secondArt","secondImg"]].forEach(([key,id])=>{const img=$(id);if(!img)return;img.src=state.profile[key]||"";img.parentElement.classList.toggle("has-image",!!state.profile[key])})}

function genericAction(a){if(a==="save"){localStorage.setItem(STORE_KEY,JSON.stringify(state));status("Saved locally.","ok");return true}if(a==="export"){exportCharacter(false);return true}if(a==="bundle"){exportCharacter(true);return true}if(a==="print"){print();return true}if(a==="new"){if(confirm("Start a new blank character? function setup(){qa("[data-bind]").forEach(el=>{el.addEventListener("input",()=>{setPath(el.dataset.bind,el.type==="checkbox"?el.checked:el.value);render();scheduleSave()})});document.body.addEventListener("input",e=>{const p=e.target.dataset.path;if(!p)return;setPath(p,e.target.type==="checkbox"?e.target.checked:e.target.value);render();scheduleSave()});document.body.addEventListener("click",e=>{const rem=e.target.closest("[data-remove-path]");if(rem){remove(rem.dataset.removePath,Number(rem.dataset.removeIndex));return}const b=e.target.closest("[data-action]");if(!b)return;const a=b.dataset.action;if(!genericAction(a))systemAction(a,b)});$("importInput")?.addEventListener("change",e=>{const f=e.target.files?.[0];if(!f)return;const r=new FileReader();r.onload=()=>{try{importObject(JSON.parse(r.result))}catch(err){status("Import failed: "+err.message,"bad")}};r.readAsText(f);e.target.value=""});$("referenceQuery")?.addEventListener("keydown",e=>{if(e.key==="Enter")referenceSearch()});setupArtwork();if(typeof systemSetup==="function")systemSetup()}

const DIE_OPTIONS=[[0,"Unskilled"],[4,"d4"],[6,"d6"],[8,"d8"],[10,"d10"],[12,"d12"]];
function swSkill(name){return state.skills.find(s=>s.name.toLowerCase()===String(name).toLowerCase())}
function skillCost(s){const core=["Athletics","Common Knowledge","Notice","Persuasion","Stealth"].includes(s.name),start=core?4:0,target=n(s.die),attr=n(state.attributes[s.attribute]);let cost=0;for(let d=start?start+2:4;d<=target;d+=2)cost+=d>attr?2:1;return cost}
function calculateSystem(){
  const fighting=n(swSkill("Fighting")?.die),parry=2+(fighting?Math.floor(fighting/2):0)+n(state.combat.parryMod)+n(state.combat.shieldParry),toughness=2+Math.floor(n(state.attributes.Vigor)/2)+n(state.combat.armor)+n(state.combat.toughnessMod),attrSpent=Object.values(state.attributes).reduce((t,d)=>t+Math.max(0,(n(d)-4)/2),0),skillSpent=state.skills.reduce((t,s)=>t+skillCost(s),0),hindrancePoints=state.hindrances.reduce((t,x)=>t+n(x.points),0),edgeCost=state.edges.reduce((t,x)=>t+n(x.cost),0);
  state.computed={parry,toughness,attrSpent,skillSpent,hindrancePoints,edgeCost,outputs:{swBennies:state.resources.bennies,swParry:parry,swToughness:toughness,swPace:state.combat.pace}};
}
function renderSwList(id,path,keys,labels){const box=$(id);if(!box)return;const arr=getPath(path);box.innerHTML=arr.map((x,i)=>'<div class="repeat-row">'+keys.map((k,j)=>'<label style="grid-column:span '+Math.max(1,Math.floor(11/keys.length))+'">'+h(labels[j])+dyn(path+"."+i+"."+k,x[k],["cost","points","pp","ap","qty","weight","pace","toughness"].includes(k)?"number":"text")+'</label>').join("")+removeButton(path,i)+'</div>').join("")}
function renderSystem(){
  const skills=$("swSkillsList");if(skills)skills.innerHTML=state.skills.map((s,i)=>'<div class="repeat-row"><label style="grid-column:span 4">Skill'+dyn("skills."+i+".name",s.name)+'</label><label style="grid-column:span 3">Linked attribute'+dynSelect("skills."+i+".attribute",s.attribute,["Agility","Smarts","Spirit","Strength","Vigor"])+'</label><label style="grid-column:span 4">Die'+dynSelect("skills."+i+".die",s.die,DIE_OPTIONS)+'<small>Creation cost '+skillCost(s)+'</small></label>'+removeButton("skills",i)+'</div>').join("");
  const val=$("swCreationValidation");if(val)val.innerHTML='<b>Standard creation:</b> Attributes '+state.computed.attrSpent+' / 5 · Skills '+state.computed.skillSpent+' / 12 · Hindrance points '+state.computed.hindrancePoints+' · Edge/benefit points recorded '+state.computed.edgeCost+'<br>'+(state.computed.attrSpent>5||state.computed.skillSpent>12?'<span class="bad">Standard point budget exceeded unless a Setting Rule changes it.</span>':'<span class="ok">Standard attribute and skill budgets are within limits.</span>');
  const tr=$("swTrackers");if(tr)tr.innerHTML='<div class="fields"><label class="field"><span>Bennies</span>'+dyn("resources.bennies",state.resources.bennies,"number",'min="0"')+'</label><label class="field"><span>Wounds</span>'+dyn("resources.wounds",state.resources.wounds,"number",'min="0" max="3"')+'</label><label class="field"><span>Fatigue</span>'+dyn("resources.fatigue",state.resources.fatigue,"number",'min="0" max="2"')+'</label></div><div class="tracker">'+["shaken","distracted","vulnerable"].map(k=>'<label><input data-path="resources.'+k+'" type="checkbox" '+(state.resources[k]?"checked":"")+'> '+k[0].toUpperCase()+k.slice(1)+'</label>').join("")+'</div>';
  renderSwList("swEdgesList","edges",["name","requirements","cost","notes"],["Edge","Requirements","Hindrance-point cost","Rules"]);
  renderSwList("swHindrancesList","hindrances",["name","severity","points","notes"],["Hindrance","Minor/Major","Points","Rules"]);
  renderSwList("swWeaponsList","weapons",["name","skill","damage","ap","range","notes"],["Weapon","Skill","Damage","AP","Range","Notes"]);
  renderSwList("swPowersList","powers",["name","skill","pp","range","duration","notes"],["Power","Arcane skill","PP","Range","Duration","Rules/modifiers"]);
  renderSwList("swAdvancesList","advances",["name","rank","type","notes"],["Advance","Rank","Type","Change"]);
  renderSwList("swGearList","gear",["name","qty","weight","notes"],["Gear","Qty","Weight","Notes"]);
  renderSwList("swVehiclesList","vehicles",["name","handling","pace","toughness","crew","notes"],["Vehicle","Handling","Pace","Toughness","Crew","Notes"]);
}
function swTraitDie(){if(state.roll.traitType==="attribute")return n(state.attributes[state.roll.trait])||4;if(state.roll.traitType==="unskilled")return 4;return n(swSkill(state.roll.trait)?.die)||4}
function swRollCore(die,target,mod,wild=true){const trait=rollExploding(die),wildRoll=wild?rollExploding(6):null,critical=wild&&trait.natural===1&&wildRoll.natural===1,traitTotal=trait.total+mod,wildTotal=wildRoll?wildRoll.total+mod:-999,total=Math.max(traitTotal,wildTotal),success=!critical&&total>=target,raises=success?Math.floor((total-target)/4):0;return{trait,wildRoll,critical,total,success,raises,traitTotal,wildTotal}}
function doSwRoll(spend=false){
  if(spend){if(!lastRoll||lastRoll.critical){status("A Critical Failure cannot be rerolled with a Benny.","bad");return}if(state.resources.bennies<=0){status("No Bennies remain.","bad");return}state.resources.bennies--}
  const die=swTraitDie(),pen=state.roll.woundPenalty?-(n(state.resources.wounds)+n(state.resources.fatigue)):0,unskilled=state.roll.traitType==="unskilled"?-2:0,mod=n(state.roll.modifier)+pen+unskilled,r=swRollCore(die,n(state.roll.target),mod,state.roll.wildCard);lastRoll=r;
  $("rollResult").textContent="Trait d"+die+": "+r.trait.parts.join("+")+" = "+r.trait.total+(r.wildRoll?"\nWild d6: "+r.wildRoll.parts.join("+")+" = "+r.wildRoll.total:"")+"\nModifier "+mod+"; keep "+r.total+" vs TN "+state.roll.target+"\n"+(r.critical?"CRITICAL FAILURE":r.success?"SUCCESS"+(r.raises?" WITH "+r.raises+" RAISE"+(r.raises===1?"":"S"):""):"FAILURE")+(r.critical?"\nBoth dice began on natural 1. This cannot be rerolled with a Benny.":"");render();scheduleSave()
}
function doSwDamage(){
  const r=parseDice(state.damage.expression,true);if(!r){$("damageResult").textContent="Use an expression like 2d6, 2d8+1, or 3d6-2.";return}const total=r.total+n(state.damage.modifier),effectiveT=Math.max(0,n(state.damage.targetToughness)-Math.min(n(state.damage.armorPiercing),n(state.combat.armor))),delta=total-effectiveT,wounds=delta>=4?Math.floor(delta/4):0,shaken=delta>=0;
  lastRoll={type:"damage",total,wounds,shaken};$("damageResult").textContent="Acing damage dice: "+r.rolls.join(", ")+" + modifier "+state.damage.modifier+" = "+total+"\nEffective Toughness "+effectiveT+" (AP only ignores applicable Armor)\n"+(delta<0?"No effect.":wounds?shaken+"; "+wounds+" Wound"+(wounds===1?"":"s")+" and Shaken.":"Shaken; no Wound from this hit.")+"\n\nA simple Shaken result against an already Shaken target can cause a Wound under the current rules."
}
function doSoak(){
  if(state.resources.bennies<=0){status("No Benny is available for a Soak roll.","bad");return}state.resources.bennies--;const pen=-(n(state.resources.wounds)+n(state.resources.fatigue)),r=swRollCore(n(state.attributes.Vigor),4,pen,true),removed=r.success?1+r.raises:0;state.damage.incomingWounds=Math.max(0,n(state.damage.incomingWounds)-removed);if(state.damage.incomingWounds===0)state.resources.shaken=false;$("damageResult").textContent="SOAK: Vigor d"+state.attributes.Vigor+" and Wild d6, penalty "+pen+"; keep "+r.total+".\n"+(r.critical?"Critical Failure.":r.success?"Success"+(r.raises?" + "+r.raises+" raise(s)":"")+" removes "+removed+" incoming Wound(s).":"Failure; no Wounds removed.")+"\nIncoming Wounds still unsoaked: "+state.damage.incomingWounds;render();scheduleSave()
}
function systemAction(a){
  if(a==="addSwSkill")add("skills",{name:"",attribute:"Agility",die:4});else if(a==="addSwEdge")add("edges",{name:"",requirements:"",cost:2,notes:""});
  else if(a==="addSwHindrance")add("hindrances",{name:"",severity:"Minor",points:1,notes:""});else if(a==="addSwWeapon")add("weapons",{name:"",skill:"Fighting",damage:"2d6",ap:0,range:"",notes:""});
  else if(a==="addSwPower")add("powers",{name:"",skill:"",pp:1,range:"",duration:"",notes:""});else if(a==="addSwAdvance")add("advances",{name:"",rank:state.profile.rank,type:"",notes:""});
  else if(a==="addSwGear")add("gear",{name:"",qty:1,weight:0,notes:""});else if(a==="addSwVehicle")add("vehicles",{name:"",handling:0,pace:0,toughness:0,crew:"",notes:""});
  else if(a==="swTraitRoll")doSwRoll(false);else if(a==="swBennyReroll")doSwRoll(true);else if(a==="gainBenny"){state.resources.bennies++;render();scheduleSave()}else if(a==="spendBenny"){if(state.resources.bennies>0)state.resources.bennies--;render();scheduleSave()}
  else if(a==="swDamage")doSwDamage();else if(a==="swSoak")doSoak();
  else if(a==="swRecoverShaken"){const oldType=state.roll.traitType,oldTrait=state.roll.trait;state.roll.traitType="attribute";state.roll.trait="Spirit";doSwRoll(false);if(lastRoll.success)state.resources.shaken=false;state.roll.traitType=oldType;state.roll.trait=oldTrait;render();scheduleSave()}
  else if(a==="swSupport")$("rollResult").textContent="SUPPORT / TEST\nSupport uses a relevant skill to help an ally; success usually grants +1 and a raise +2, within the stacking limit. A Test targets an opponent's linked attribute and can make them Distracted or Vulnerable; a raise can also make them Shaken. State the fiction and use the exact skill and resistance.";
  else if(a==="swInitiative"){const ranks=["2","3","4","5","6","7","8","9","10","Jack","Queen","King","Ace"],suits=["Clubs","Diamonds","Hearts","Spades"],card=Math.random()<2/54?"Joker":ranks[Math.floor(Math.random()*ranks.length)]+" of "+suits[Math.floor(Math.random()*suits.length)];$("damageResult").textContent="ACTION CARD: "+card+(card==="Joker"?"\nAct whenever you choose this round; gain the Joker bonus to Trait and damage rolls, and reshuffle after the round.":"\nResolve from Ace down to 2; ties use suit order. Edges, Hindrances, and spending Bennies may alter the draw.")}
}

init();

<script id="characterSheetCustomizationScript">
(()=>{
'use strict';
const PRESETS={
  'style-1':{primary:'#2fbed8',secondary:'#ca6309',surface:'#effbff',text:'#0b3f43',background:'#000305'},
  'style-2':{primary:'#ca6309',secondary:'#d7aa63',surface:'#fff3e8',text:'#3a200b',background:'#120a05'},
  'style-3':{primary:'#3d9fa0',secondary:'#8a9a5b',surface:'#e8fff9',text:'#1a3029',background:'#07090a'},
  'style-4':{primary:'#595e8d',secondary:'#7b75c9',surface:'#f1f1ff',text:'#161344',background:'#100b18'},
  'style-5':{primary:'#6d858e',secondary:'#a6bec5',surface:'#f7f7f7',text:'#11161b',background:'#11161b'},
  'style-6':{primary:'#c97a00',secondary:'#357c8f',surface:'#fff9df',text:'#292112',background:'#18272c'}
};
const DEFAULT_COLORS={primary:'#2f5ea8',secondary:'#c9912f',surface:'#fff8e8',text:'#171a20',background:'#101827'};
const KEY='character-sheet-appearance-v2:'+(location.pathname.split('/').pop()||document.title);
const root=document.documentElement;
const byId=id=>document.getElementById(id);
const bubble=byId('characterSheetBubble');
const toggle=byId('characterSheetBubbleToggle');
const panel=byId('characterSheetBubblePanel');
const feedback=byId('csFeedback');
let state={preset:'default',colors:null,backgroundMode:'default',backgroundImage:'',backgroundUrl:'',overlay:46,position:null};
let suppressClick=false;

function announce(message){feedback.textContent=message;clearTimeout(announce.timer);announce.timer=setTimeout(()=>{if(feedback.textContent===message)feedback.textContent='';},3500)}
function load(){try{const embedded=window.__EMBEDDED_CHARACTER_SHEET_APPEARANCE__;if(embedded&&typeof embedded==='object')state=Object.assign(state,embedded);else{const saved=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(KEY)||'null');if(saved&&typeof saved==='object')state=Object.assign(state,saved);}}catch(_){}}
function save(){try{localStorage.setItem(KEY,JSON.stringify(state));}catch(_){announce('This image is too large to save locally, but it remains active for this session.')}}
function setVar(name,value){root.style.setProperty(name,value)}
function activeColors(){return state.colors||PRESETS[state.preset]||DEFAULT_COLORS}
function applyColors(){
  const enabled=state.preset!=='default'||!!state.colors;
  if(!enabled){root.removeAttribute('data-cs-style');['--cs-primary','--cs-secondary','--cs-surface','--cs-text','--cs-background'].forEach(v=>root.style.removeProperty(v));return;}
  const c=activeColors();root.setAttribute('data-cs-style','active');
  setVar('--cs-primary',c.primary);setVar('--cs-secondary',c.secondary);setVar('--cs-surface',c.surface);setVar('--cs-text',c.text);setVar('--cs-background',c.background);
}
function cssUrl(value){return 'url('+JSON.stringify(value)+')'}
function applyBackground(){
  root.style.setProperty('--cs-overlay',String(Math.max(0,Math.min(85,Number(state.overlay)||0))/100));
  root.removeAttribute('data-cs-background');
  if(state.backgroundMode==='custom'&&state.backgroundImage){root.setAttribute('data-cs-background','custom');root.style.setProperty('--cs-background-image',cssUrl(state.backgroundImage));}
  else if(state.backgroundMode==='none'){root.setAttribute('data-cs-background','none');root.style.removeProperty('--cs-background-image');}
  else root.style.removeProperty('--cs-background-image');
}
function applyPosition(){
  if(!state.position)return;
  const maxX=Math.max(0,innerWidth-bubble.offsetWidth),maxY=Math.max(0,innerHeight-bubble.offsetHeight);
  bubble.style.left=Math.min(maxX,Math.max(0,state.position.x))+'px';bubble.style.top=Math.min(maxY,Math.max(0,state.position.y))+'px';bubble.style.right='auto';bubble.style.bottom='auto';positionPanel();
}
function syncControls(){
  byId('csStyleSelect').value=state.preset in PRESETS?state.preset:'default';
  const c=activeColors();byId('csPrimaryColor').value=c.primary;byId('csSecondaryColor').value=c.secondary;byId('csSurfaceColor').value=c.surface;byId('csTextColor').value=c.text;byId('csBackgroundColor').value=c.background;
  byId('csBackgroundUrl').value=state.backgroundUrl||'';byId('csOverlayRange').value=state.overlay;byId('csOverlayValue').textContent=state.overlay+'%';
}
function applyAll(){applyColors();applyBackground();syncControls();requestAnimationFrame(applyPosition)}
function sectionLabel(el,index){const heading=el.querySelector(':scope > header h1,:scope > header h2,:scope > header h3,:scope > .module-hd h1,:scope > .module-hd h2,:scope > .module-hd h3,h1,h2,h3');return (heading&&heading.textContent.trim())||el.getAttribute('aria-label')||('Section '+index)}
function buildJumpList(){
  const select=byId('csJumpSelect');const found=[];const seen=new Set();
  const candidates=document.querySelectorAll('main section[id],main .module[id],.layout .module[id],.sheet .panel[id],.sheet section[id]');
  candidates.forEach((el,i)=>{if(!seen.has(el.id)){seen.add(el.id);found.push({id:el.id,label:sectionLabel(el,i+1)})}});
  select.innerHTML='<option value="__top">Top of Sheet</option>'+found.map(x=>'<option value="'+x.id.replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;')+'">'+x.label.replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;')+'</option>').join('')+'<option value="__bottom">Bottom of Sheet</option>';
}
function jump(){const value=byId('csJumpSelect').value;if(value==='__top')scrollTo({top:0,behavior:'smooth'});else if(value==='__bottom')scrollTo({top:document.documentElement.scrollHeight,behavior:'smooth'});else{const target=byId(value);if(target)target.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth',block:'start'});}closePanel()}
function openPanel(){bubble.classList.add('open');toggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded','true');positionPanel()}
function closePanel(){bubble.classList.remove('open');toggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded','false')}
function positionPanel(){const r=bubble.getBoundingClientRect();bubble.classList.toggle('panel-right',r.left<350);bubble.classList.toggle('panel-below',r.top<360)}
function usePreset(value){state.preset=value;state.colors=null;applyColors();syncControls();save();announce(value==='default'?'Sheet default colors restored.':value.replace('-',' ').replace(/^./,s=>s.toUpperCase())+' applied.')}
function readColors(){return{primary:byId('csPrimaryColor').value,secondary:byId('csSecondaryColor').value,surface:byId('csSurfaceColor').value,text:byId('csTextColor').value,background:byId('csBackgroundColor').value}}
function applyEditedColors(){if(state.preset==='default')state.preset='style-1';state.colors=readColors();applyColors();syncControls();save();announce('Custom colors applied to '+state.preset.replace('-',' ')+'.')}
function processImage(file){
  if(!file||!file.type.startsWith('image/'))return announce('Choose an image file.');
  const reader=new FileReader();reader.onload=()=>{const image=new Image();image.onload=()=>{const max=2200,scale=Math.min(1,max/Math.max(image.naturalWidth,image.naturalHeight));const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');canvas.width=Math.max(1,Math.round(image.naturalWidth*scale));canvas.height=Math.max(1,Math.round(image.naturalHeight*scale));canvas.getContext('2d').drawImage(image,0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);const type=file.type==='image/png'?'image/png':'image/jpeg';state.backgroundImage=canvas.toDataURL(type,type==='image/jpeg'?.88:undefined);state.backgroundUrl='';state.backgroundMode='custom';applyBackground();syncControls();save();announce('Background image applied.');};image.onerror=()=>announce('The image could not be read.');image.src=reader.result;};reader.readAsDataURL(file);
}
function bind(){
  byId('csJumpGo').addEventListener('click',jump);byId('csJumpSelect').addEventListener('change',jump);
  byId('csStyleSelect').addEventListener('change',e=>usePreset(e.target.value));
  ['csPrimaryColor','csSecondaryColor','csSurfaceColor','csTextColor','csBackgroundColor'].forEach(id=>byId(id).addEventListener('input',applyEditedColors));
  byId('csRestoreStyle').addEventListener('click',()=>usePreset(state.preset));
  byId('csBackgroundFile').addEventListener('change',e=>{processImage(e.target.files&&e.target.files[0]);e.target.value=''});
  byId('csApplyBackgroundUrl').addEventListener('click',()=>{const url=byId('csBackgroundUrl').value.trim();if(!url)return announce('Paste an image URL first.');state.backgroundImage=url;state.backgroundUrl=url;state.backgroundMode='custom';applyBackground();save();announce('Background URL applied.');});
  byId('csNoBackground').addEventListener('click',()=>{state.backgroundMode='none';state.backgroundImage='';state.backgroundUrl='';applyBackground();syncControls();save();announce('Background image removed.');});
  byId('csDefaultBackground').addEventListener('click',()=>{state.backgroundMode='default';state.backgroundImage='';state.backgroundUrl='';applyBackground();syncControls();save();announce('Sheet default background restored.');});
  byId('csOverlayRange').addEventListener('input',e=>{state.overlay=Number(e.target.value);byId('csOverlayValue').textContent=state.overlay+'%';applyBackground();save()});
  byId('csResetAll').addEventListener('click',()=>{state={preset:'default',colors:null,backgroundMode:'default',backgroundImage:'',backgroundUrl:'',overlay:46,position:null};localStorage.removeItem(KEY);root.removeAttribute('data-cs-style');root.removeAttribute('data-cs-background');['--cs-primary','--cs-secondary','--cs-surface','--cs-text','--cs-background','--cs-background-image','--cs-overlay'].forEach(v=>root.style.removeProperty(v));bubble.removeAttribute('style');syncControls();positionPanel();announce('Appearance and bubble position reset.');});
  toggle.addEventListener('click',()=>{if(suppressClick){suppressClick=false;return}bubble.classList.contains('open')?closePanel():openPanel()});
  document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Escape')closePanel()});
  document.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(bubble.classList.contains('open')&&!bubble.contains(e.target))closePanel()});
  let start=null,moved=false;
  toggle.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{if(e.button!==undefined&&e.button!==0)return;start={x:e.clientX,y:e.clientY,left:bubble.getBoundingClientRect().left,top:bubble.getBoundingClientRect().top};moved=false;toggle.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId)});
  toggle.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{if(!start)return;const dx=e.clientX-start.x,dy=e.clientY-start.y;if(Math.hypot(dx,dy)>5)moved=true;if(!moved)return;const x=Math.max(0,Math.min(innerWidth-bubble.offsetWidth,start.left+dx)),y=Math.max(0,Math.min(innerHeight-bubble.offsetHeight,start.top+dy));bubble.style.left=x+'px';bubble.style.top=y+'px';bubble.style.right='auto';bubble.style.bottom='auto';positionPanel()});
  toggle.addEventListener('pointerup',e=>{if(!start)return;try{toggle.releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId)}catch(_){}if(moved){const r=bubble.getBoundingClientRect();state.position={x:Math.round(r.left),y:Math.round(r.top)};save();suppressClick=true;}start=null});
  addEventListener('resize',()=>{applyPosition();positionPanel()});
}
function init(){load();buildJumpList();applyAll();bind();window.__CHARACTER_SHEET_APPEARANCE_API__={getState:()=>JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(state))}}
if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',init,{once:true});else init();
})();

const DND_XP=[0,300,900,2700,6500,14000,23000,34000,48000,64000,85000,100000,120000,140000,165000,195000,225000,265000,305000,355000];
function totalLevel(){try{return typeof window.totalLevel==='function'?window.totalLevel():stateRef().classes?.reduce((n,c)=>n+number(c.level),0)||0}catch(_){return stateRef().classes?.reduce((n,c)=>n+number(c.level),0)||0}}
function swadeRank(n){return n>=16?'Legendary':n>=12?'Heroic':n>=8?'Veteran':n>=4?'Seasoned':'Novice'}
function fateResources(){return number(stateRef().skills?.find(x=>x.name==='Resources')?.rating)}
function cocCredit(){return number(stateRef().skills?.find(x=>x.name==='Credit Rating')?.value)}
function cocBand(v){return v<=0?'Penniless':v<=9?'Poor':v<=49?'Average':v<=89?'Wealthy':v<=98?'Rich':'Super rich'}
function bitdTrackInfo(){const s=stateRef(),t=$('hyperProgressTarget')?.value||'playbook',threshold=t==='playbook'?8:t==='crew'?10:6,current=t==='crew'?number(s.crew?.crewXp):number(s.xp?.[t]);return{t,threshold,current}}
function progressionInfo(){const s=stateRef(),a=auto();switch(CFG.progression){case'dnd-xp':{const l=Math.max(1,totalLevel()),xp=number(s.profile?.xp),next=l>=20?null:DND_XP[l];return{current:xp,next:next===null?'Maximum level':Math.max(0,next-xp)+' XP',status:next!==null&&xp>=next?'Level ready':'Level '+l,prompt:next===null?'Level 20 reached.':`Standard threshold for level ${l+1}: ${next.toLocaleString()} cumulative XP.`}}case'pf2e-xp':{const xp=number(a.progression.xp);return{current:xp+' XP',next:Math.max(0,1000-xp)+' XP',status:xp>=1000?'Level ready':'Level '+number(s.profile?.level),prompt:'Pathfinder uses 1,000 XP per level; excess XP carries over.'}}case'pbta-xp':{const cur=number(s.xp?.current),max=Math.max(1,number(s.xp?.max)||5);return{current:`${cur}/${max}`,next:Math.max(0,max-cur)+' marks',status:cur>=max?'Advance ready':'Tracking',prompt:'The exact playbook defines what an advance can buy. The sheet never invents a universal PbtA level system.'}}case'swade-advances':{const n=Math.max(number(a.progression.advances),s.advances?.length||0),rank=swadeRank(n),next=n<4?4:n<8?8:n<12?12:n<16?16:null;return{current:n+' Advances',next:next?`${next-n} to ${swadeRank(next)}`:'Legendary',status:rank,prompt:'Each recorded Advance automatically recalculates Rank and opens the improvement checklist.'}}case'bitd-xp':{const x=bitdTrackInfo();return{current:`${x.current}/${x.threshold}`,next:Math.max(0,x.threshold-x.current)+' XP',status:x.current>=x.threshold?'Advance ready':x.t,prompt:'Playbook fills at 8; Insight, Prowess, and Resolve at 6; crew XP fills at 10.'}}case'narrative-levels':{const lvl=number(s.profile?.level)||1,ss=number(a.progression.sessionsSinceLevel),target=Math.max(1,number(a.progression.sessionsPerLevel)||3);return{current:`Level ${lvl}`,next:lvl>=10?'Maximum level':`${Math.max(0,target-ss)} session(s)`,status:ss>=target&&lvl<10?'Milestone suggested':`Tier ${lvl===1?1:lvl<=4?2:lvl<=7?3:4}`,prompt:'The party levels together when the Guide declares a narrative milestone, commonly around every three sessions.'}}case'character-points':{const earned=number(a.progression.earned),spent=number(s.computed?.outputs?.gurpsSpent),budget=number(s.budget);const available=budget+earned-spent;return{current:`${available} available`,next:`${earned} earned`,status:available<0?'Overspent':'Balanced',prompt:`Starting budget ${budget}; earned ${earned}; calculated spent ${spent}; available ${available}.`}}case'development':{const checked=(s.skills||[]).filter(x=>x.checked&&x.name!=='Cthulhu Mythos');return{current:checked.length+' checked',next:checked.length?'Development phase':'Use skills',status:cocBand(cocCredit()),prompt:`Credit Rating ${cocCredit()} is ${cocBand(cocCredit())}. Checked skills are resolved with externally supplied d100 and d10 results.`}}case'milestones':{const m=a.progression.milestones,total=number(m.minor)+number(m.significant)+number(m.major);return{current:total+' awarded',next:'Story-driven',status:`Resources ${fateResources()}`,prompt:'Minor, significant, and major milestones change the character according to the fiction; Fate Core does not use XP levels.'}}default:return{current:'—',next:'—',status:'Ready',prompt:''}}
}

features.</li></ul>`;case'pf2e-xp':return `<p>Advance from level ${number(s.profile?.level)} to ${Math.min(20,number(s.profile?.level)+1)} and carry excess XP.</p><ul><li>Increase level-based proficiency automatically through the sheet.</li><li>Recalculate HP, AC, saves, Perception, skills, class DC, spell DC, and strike modifiers.</li><li>Review the level’s class/skill/general/ancestry feat and ability-boost schedule.</li><li>Update spell ranks, slots, equipment expectations, and retraining choices.</li></ul>`;case'pbta-xp':return `<p>The XP track is full.</p><ul><li>Choose an improvement from the exact game and playbook.</li><li>Record any stat increase, new move, cross-playbook move, relationship change, retirement, or playbook change.</li><li>The sheet subtracts one full XP track and preserves carryover.</li></ul>`;case'swade-advances':return `<p>Record one SWADE Advance.</p><ul><li>Gain an eligible Edge; or increase one skill at/above its linked Attribute one die type.</li><li>Increase two skills below linked Attributes; or buy a new skill at d4.</li><li>Increase one Attribute when allowed for the current Rank.</li><li>Check Rank, Trait, setting, ancestry, and Arcane Background prerequisites.</li></ul>`;case'bitd-xp':{const x=bitdTrackInfo();return `<p>${html(x.t)} XP is full.</p><ul><li>${x.t==='playbook'?'Choose a new special ability.':x.t==='crew'?'Choose one crew special ability or mark two crew upgrade boxes.':'Add one action dot under '+html(x.t)+'.'}</li><li>Subtract one full track and preserve overflow.</li><li>Record the choice in the appropriate ability, action, or crew area.</li></ul>`}case'narrative-levels':{const n=Math.min(10,number(s.profile?.level)+1);return `<p>Advance the party to Daggerheart level ${n}.</p><ul><li>Choose two valid advancement slots.</li><li>Increase Major and Severe damage thresholds by 1.</li><li>Gain one legal domain card at or below the new level.</li>${[2,5,8].includes(n)?'<li>Tier achievement: gain a +2 Experience and increase Proficiency by 1.</li>':''}<li>Recalculate Evasion, HP, Stress, Proficiency, thresholds, and card loadout.</li></ul>`}case'character-points':return `<p>Spend earned character points only when training, story, and campaign permissions support the change.</p><ul><li>Improve or learn skills.</li><li>Raise attributes or secondary characteristics with approval.</li><li>Buy new advantages or buy off disadvantages when justified.</li><li>The point ledger automatically recalculates spent and available points.</li></ul>`;case'development':return `<p>Resolve checked skills using d100 results supplied by the table or future portal.</p><ul><li>If the d100 result is above the current skill, add the supplied d10 improvement.</li><li>If the result is equal to or below the skill, no increase occurs.</li><li>Erase the check after resolving it.</li><li>Cthulhu Mythos does not use ordinary improvement checks.</li></ul><div class="hyper-manual-roll"><label>Improvement check d100 result <input id="cocImproveRoll" type="number" min="1" max="100" value="50"></label><label>Improvement d10 result <input id="cocImproveGain" type="number" min="1" max="10" value="5"></label></div>`;case'milestones':{const t=$('hyperProgressTarget')?.value||'minor';const rows={minor:['Switch two skill ratings.','Replace one Average skill.','Replace a stunt.','Rename a non-high-concept aspect.','Clear stress and begin consequence recovery as fiction allows.'],significant:['Gain one skill point while preserving a valid skill column.','Also receive the benefits of a minor milestone.'],major:['Gain one Refresh.','Advance a skill beyond the current cap if the campaign allows.','Rename the high concept if the story supports it.','Recover the ability to take an extreme consequence after rewriting it.','Also receive significant and minor milestone benefits.']}[t];return `<p>Apply a ${html(t)} milestone.</p><ul>${rows.map(x=>'<li>'+html(x)+'</li>').join('')}</ul>`}default:return'<p>Record the advancement in the system-specific sections.</p>'}}
function recordProgress(){const a=auto(),s=stateRef(),amount=Math.max(0,number($('hyperProgressAmount')?.value||1)),memo=$('hyperProgressMemo')?.value||'';if(CFG.progression==='development'){openModal('Call of Cthulhu Development Phase',advancementChecklist(),'coc-development-'+(s.skills||[]).filter(x=>x.checked).map(x=>x.name).join('|'),true);return}if(CFG.progression==='milestones'){const t=$('hyperProgressTarget').value;a.progression.milestones[t]=number(a.progression.milestones[t])+Math.max(1,amount);history('progression',Math.max(1,amount),memo,{target:t});if(s.advancement)s.advancement.milestone=t[0].toUpperCase()+t.slice(1);openModal(`${t[0].toUpperCase()+t.slice(1)} Milestone`,advancementChecklist(),`fate-${t}-${a.progression.milestones[t]}`,true)}else if(CFG.progression==='dnd-xp'){s.profile.xp=number(s.profile.xp)+amount;history('progression',amount,memo,{target:'xp'})}else if(CFG.progression==='pf2e-xp'){a.progression.xp=number(a.progression.xp)+amount;history('progression',amount,memo,{target:'xp'})}else if(CFG.progression==='pbta-xp'){s.xp.current=number(s.xp.current)+amount;history('progression',amount,memo,{target:'xp'})}else if(CFG.progression==='swade-advances'){const count=Math.max(1,Math.floor(amount));a.progression.advances=Math.max(number(a.progression.advances),s.advances?.length||0)+count;for(let i=0;i<count;i++)s.advances.push({name:`Advance ${s.advances.length+1}`,rank:swadeRank(a.progression.advances),type:'',notes:memo});s.profile.rank=swadeRank(a.progression.advances);history('progression',count,memo,{target:'advance'});openModal('SWADE Advance Recorded',advancementChecklist(),`swade-${a.progression.advances}`,true)}else if(CFG.progression==='bitd-xp'){const x=bitdTrackInfo();if(x.t==='crew')s.crew.crewXp=number(s.crew.crewXp)+amount;else s.xp[x.t]=number(s.xp[x.t])+amount;history('progression',amount,memo,{target:x.t})}else if(CFG.progression==='narrative-levels'){a.progression.sessionsSinceLevel=number(a.progression.sessionsSinceLevel)+amount;history('progression',amount,memo,{target:'sessions'})}else if(CFG.progression==='character-points'){a.progression.earned=number(a.progression.earned)+amount;history('progression',amount,memo,{target:'character-points'})}rerender();checkProgression(true)}
function applyProgression(){const a=auto(),s=stateRef();if(CFG.progression==='dnd-xp'){$('levelUpBtn')?.click();closeModal();return}if(CFG.progression==='pf2e-xp'){if(number(a.progression.xp)<1000||number(s.profile.level)>=20)return;const old=number(s.profile.level);a.progression.xp-=1000;s.profile.level=old+1;history('progression',1,`Advanced to level ${s.profile.level}`,{target:'level'});if(s.hp){const gain=number(s.hp.classPerLevel)+number(s.attributes?.con);s.hp.current=number(s.hp.current)+Math.max(1,gain)}a.progression.lastPopupKey=''}else if(CFG.progression==='pbta-xp'){const max=Math.max(1,number(s.xp.max)||5);if(number(s.xp.current)<max)return;s.xp.current-=max;s.advancements=s.advancements||[];s.advancements.push({name:'Choose exact-game advancement',notes:'XP track completed '+new Date().toLocaleDateString()});history('progression',1,'XP track converted to an advancement',{target:'advance'});a.progression.lastPopupKey=''}else if(CFG.progression==='bitd-xp'){const x=bitdTrackInfo();if(x.current<x.threshold)return;if(x.t==='crew')s.crew.crewXp-=x.threshold;else s.xp[x.t]-=x.threshold;history('progression',1,`${x.t} track converted to an advance`,{target:x.t});a.progression.lastPopupKey=''}else if(CFG.progression==='narrative-levels'){const lvl=number(s.profile.level);if(lvl>=10)return;const next=lvl+1;s.profile.level=next;s.thresholds.majorMod=number(s.thresholds.majorMod)+1;s.thresholds.severeMod=number(s.thresholds.severeMod)+1;if([2,5,8].includes(next)){s.resources.proficiency=number(s.resources.proficiency)+1;s.experiences=s.experiences||[];s.experiences.push({name:'New Tier Experience',value:2})}a.progression.sessionsSinceLevel=0;history('progression',1,`Advanced to Daggerheart level ${next}`,{target:'level'});a.progression.lastPopupKey=''}else if(CFG.progression==='development'){const skillName=$('cocDevelopmentSkill')?.value,skill=(s.skills||[]).find(x=>x.name===skillName);if(!skill)return;const roll=clamp(number($('cocImproveRoll')?.value),1,100),gain=clamp(number($('cocImproveGain')?.value),1,10),old=number(skill.value),success=roll>old;if(success)skill.value=clamp(old+gain,0,99);skill.checked=false;history('progression',success?gain:0,`${skill.name}: d100 ${roll} vs ${old}${success?`, +${gain}`:', no increase'}`,{target:skill.name,roll,gain,success});a.progression.lastPopupKey=''}else if(CFG.progression==='milestones'){history('progression',0,'Milestone checklist acknowledged',{target:$('hyperProgressTarget')?.value||'minor'})}else if(CFG.progression==='character-points'){history('progression',0,'Character point spending checklist reviewed',{target:'character-points'})}closeModal();rerender();checkProgression(false)}
function checkProgression(force=false){const s=stateRef(),a=auto();if(!$('hyperProgressModal'))return;if(CFG.progression==='dnd-xp'){const l=Math.max(1,totalLevel()),next=l>=20?Infinity:DND_XP[l];if(number(s.profile?.xp)>=next)openModal(`Level ${l+1} Ready`,advancementChecklist(),`dnd-${l+1}-${number(s.profile.xp)}`,force)}else if(CFG.progression==='pf2e-xp'&&number(a.progression.xp)>=1000&&number(s.profile.level)<20)openModal(`Pathfinder Level ${number(s.profile.level)+1} Ready`,advancementChecklist(),`pf-${number(s.profile.level)+1}-${Math.floor(number(a.progression.xp)/1000)}`,force);else if(CFG.progression==='pbta-xp'&&number(s.xp.current)>=Math.max(1,number(s.xp.max)))openModal('PbtA Advancement Ready',advancementChecklist(),`pbta-${number(s.xp.current)}-${number(s.xp.max)}`,force);else if(CFG.progression==='bitd-xp'){const x=bitdTrackInfo();if(x.current>=x.threshold)openModal(`${x.t[0].toUpperCase()+x.t.slice(1)} Advance Ready`,advancementChecklist(),`bitd-${x.t}-${x.current}`,force)}else if(CFG.progression==='narrative-levels'&&number(a.progression.sessionsSinceLevel)>=Math.max(1,number(a.progression.sessionsPerLevel))&&number(s.profile.level)<10)openModal(`Daggerheart Level ${number(s.profile.level)+1} Suggested`,advancementChecklist(),`dh-${number(s.profile.level)+1}-${number(a.progression.sessionsSinceLevel)}`,force);else if(CFG.progression==='development'&&(s.skills||[]).some(x=>x.checked&&x.name!=='Cthulhu Mythos')&&force)openModal('Development Phase Ready',advancementChecklist(),`coc-${(s.skills||[]).filter(x=>x.checked).length}`,force)}
function wallet(){const a=auto(),s=stateRef(),w=a.currency.wallet;if(CFG.currency==='dnd-coins')return s.coins||(s.coins={cp:0,sp:0,ep:0,gp:0,pp:0});if(CFG.currency==='bitd-coin')return{get coin(){return number(s.resources.coin)},set coin(v){s.resources.coin=v},get stash(){return number(s.resources.stash)},set stash(v){s.resources.stash=v},get crewCoin(){return number(s.crew.coin)},set crewCoin(v){s.crew.coin=v}};return w}
function ratios(){switch(CFG.currency){case'dnd-coins':return{cp:1,sp:10,ep:50,gp:100,pp:1000};case'pf2e-coins':return{cp:1,sp:10,gp:100,pp:1000};case'daggerheart-gold':return{coin:1,handful:10,bag:100,chest:1000};default:return null}}
function currencyUnit(){return $('hyperCurrencyUnit')?.value||auto().currency.unit||({'dnd-coins':'cp','pf2e-coins':'cp','daggerheart-gold':'coin','bitd-coin':'coin','coc-finance':'cash','fate-abstract':'story funds'})[CFG.currency]||'unit'}
function currencyTotal(){const w=wallet(),r=ratios();if(r){const total=Object.entries(r).reduce((n,[k,m])=>n+number(w[k])*m,0);if(CFG.currency==='dnd-coins')return`${total.toLocaleString()} cp equivalent · ${(total/100).toFixed(2)} gp`;if(CFG.currency==='pf2e-coins')return`${total.toLocaleString()} cp equivalent · ${(total/100).toFixed(2)} gp`;return`${total.toLocaleString()} coins · ${(total/10).toFixed(1)} handfuls · ${(total/100).toFixed(2)} bags · ${(total/1000).toFixed(3)} chests`}if(CFG.currency==='bitd-coin')return`${number(w.coin)} carried coin · ${number(w.stash)} stash · ${number(w.crewCoin)} crew coin`;if(CFG.currency==='coc-finance')return`Cash ${number(w.cash).toLocaleString()} · Assets ${number(w.assets).toLocaleString()} · Spending ${number(w.spending).toLocaleString()} · Credit ${cocCredit()} (${cocBand(cocCredit())})`;if(CFG.currency==='fate-abstract')return`Resources ${fateResources()} · ${number(w.balance).toLocaleString()} ${currencyUnit()} (optional exact funds)`;return`${number(w.balance).toLocaleString()} ${currencyUnit()}`}
function syncCurrencyInputs(){const w=wallet();qa('[data-currency-key]').forEach(el=>{const k=el.dataset.currencyKey;el.value=number(w[k])});if($('hyperCurrencyUnit'))$('hyperCurrencyUnit').value=auto().currency.unit}
function currencyTransaction(sign){const w=wallet(),key=$('hyperCurrencyDenom').value,amount=Math.max(0,number($('hyperCurrencyAmount').value)),delta=sign*amount,memo=$('hyperCurrencyMemo').value;if(sign<0&&number(w[key])<amount){openModal('Insufficient Currency',`<p>The ${html(key)} balance is ${number(w[key])}; this transaction needs ${amount}.</p>`,'',true);return}w[key]=number(w[key])+delta;history('currency',delta,memo,{denomination:key});schedule();renderCurrency();renderLedgers();runAudit()}
function normalizeCurrency(){const w=wallet(),r=ratios();if(!r){if(CFG.currency==='bitd-coin'){openModal('Blades Coin & Stash',`<ul><li>Deposit: 1 coin becomes 1 stash.</li><li>Withdrawal: spend 2 stash to gain 1 coin.</li><li>Carried coin above 4 normally consumes load or must be stored.</li></ul>`,'',true)}return}let total=Math.round(Object.entries(r).reduce((n,[k,m])=>n+number(w[k])*m,0));Object.entries(r).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]).forEach(([k,m])=>{w[k]=Math.floor(total/m);total%=m});history('currency',0,'Normalized denominations',{denomination:'all'});schedule();renderCurrency();renderLedgers();runAudit()}
function renderCurrency(){if(!$('hyperCurrencyTotal'))return;syncCurrencyInputs();$('hyperCurrencyTotal').textContent=currencyTotal()}
function renderLedgers(){const a=auto(),render=(id,rows)=>{const el=$(id);if(!el)return;el.innerHTML=rows.length?rows.slice(0,80).map(e=>`<div class="hyper-ledger-entry"><span>${new Date(e.at).toLocaleString()}</span><span>${html(e.memo||e.target||e.type)}</span><b>${number(e.amount)>=0?'+':''}${number(e.amount)}${e.denomination?' '+html(e.denomination):''}</b></div>`).join(''):'<div class="hyper-note">No entries yet.</div>'};render('hyperCurrencyLedger',a.currency.history);render('hyperProgressLedger',a.progression.history)}
function runAudit(){const s=stateRef(),a=auto(),items=[];const good=t=>items.push({kind:'good',text:t}),warn=t=>items.push({kind:'warn',text:t});switch(CFG.id){case'fate-core':{const refresh=number(s.computed?.outputs?.fateRefresh),fp=number(s.fatePoints);refresh<1?warn('Refresh is below the normal minimum of 1.'):good(`Refresh ${refresh}; Fate points ${fp}.`);s.computed?.pyramidValid?good('Skill pyramid/column validation passes.'):warn('Skill pyramid/column validation needs attention.');break}case'gurps-4e':{const spent=number(s.computed?.outputs?.gurpsSpent),available=number(s.budget)+number(a.progression.earned)-spent;available<0?warn(`Point budget overspent by ${Math.abs(available)}.`):good(`${available} character points available.`);number(s.combat?.hpCurrent)<=number(s.computed?.outputs?.gurpsHP)/3&&warn('Current HP is at or below one-third; apply injury and movement rules.');break}case'coc-7e':{good(`Credit Rating ${cocCredit()}: ${cocBand(cocCredit())}.`);const checked=(s.skills||[]).filter(x=>x.checked&&x.name!=='Cthulhu Mythos').length;checked?warn(`${checked} skill improvement check(s) are ready.`):good('No unresolved skill improvement checks.');number(s.resources?.sanCurrent)<number(s.resources?.sanLossStart)-Math.floor(number(s.resources?.sanLossStart)/5)&&warn('Session SAN loss may have reached the indefinite-insanity threshold.');break}case'daggerheart':{const r=s.resources||{};number(r.hope)>number(r.hopeMax)?warn('Hope exceeds maximum.'):good(`Hope ${number(r.hope)}/${number(r.hopeMax)}.`);number(r.hpMarked)>number(r.hpMax)&&warn('Marked HP exceeds available HP slots.');number(r.stressMarked)>number(r.stressMax)&&warn('Marked Stress exceeds available Stress slots.');number(wallet().chest)>1&&warn('More than one chest is marked as carried.');break}case'pathfinder-2e-remastered':{number(a.progression.xp)>=1000?warn('At least one level is ready to apply.'):good(`${number(a.progression.xp)} / 1000 XP.`);number(s.dying)>=4?warn('Dying value is at the normal death threshold.'):good(`Dying ${number(s.dying)}, Wounded ${number(s.wounded)}, Doomed ${number(s.doomed)}.`);break}case'pbta-adapter':{const max=Math.max(1,number(s.xp?.max));number(s.xp?.current)>=max?warn('XP track is full.'):good(`XP ${number(s.xp?.current)}/${max}.`);number(s.harm?.current)>=number(s.harm?.max)&&warn('Harm/condition track is full.');break}case'swade':{const n=Math.max(number(a.progression.advances),s.advances?.length||0),rank=swadeRank(n);s.profile.rank!==rank?warn(`Rank should be ${rank} from ${n} Advances.`):good(`${n} Advances: ${rank}.`);number(s.resources?.wounds)>=3&&warn('Three Wounds marked; check Incapacitation procedure.');break}case'blades-in-the-dark':{number(s.resources?.coin)>4?warn('More than 4 coin carried; excess normally costs load or needs storage.'):good(`${number(s.resources?.coin)} carried coin.`);number(s.resources?.stress)>=number(s.resources?.stressMax)&&warn('Stress track is full; resolve trauma.');['playbook','insight','prowess','resolve'].forEach(k=>{const max=k==='playbook'?8:6;if(number(s.xp?.[k])>=max)warn(`${k} XP track is full.`)});number(s.crew?.crewXp)>=10&&warn('Crew XP track is full.');break}case'dnd-5e-5.5e':{const l=Math.max(1,totalLevel()),xp=number(s.profile?.xp),next=l>=20?Infinity:DND_XP[l];xp>=next&&warn(`Enough XP for level ${l+1}.`);good(`Level ${l}; proficiency bonus ${2+Math.floor(Math.max(0,l-1)/4)}.`);const coins=s.coins||{},cp=number(coins.cp)+10*number(coins.sp)+50*number(coins.ep)+100*number(coins.gp)+1000*number(coins.pp);good(`${(cp/100).toFixed(2)} gp equivalent.`);break}}
 a.audit.lastRun=now();const ul=$('hyperMathAudit');if(ul)ul.innerHTML=items.map(x=>`<li class="${x.kind}">${html(x.text)}</li>`).join('');return items}
function renderProgress(){if(!$('hyperProgressCurrent'))return;const i=progressionInfo();$('hyperProgressCurrent').textContent=i.current;$('hyperProgressNext').textContent=i.next;$('hyperProgressStatus').textContent=i.status;$('hyperProgressPrompt').textContent=i.prompt;if(CFG.progression==='development'){const sel=$('cocDevelopmentSkill'),current=sel?.value;sel.innerHTML=(stateRef().skills||[]).filter(x=>x.checked&&x.name!=='Cthulhu Mythos').map(x=>`<option value="${html(x.name)}">${html(x.name)} (${number(x.value)}%)</option>`).join('')||'<option value="">No checked skills</option>';if(current&&qa('option',sel).some(o=>o.value===current))sel.value=current}if(CFG.progression==='narrative-levels'){$('hyperProgressAmount').value=Math.max(1,number($('hyperProgressAmount').value)||1)}runAudit()}
function renderPortal(){const el=$('hyperPortalContract');if(!el)return;const snap=portalSnapshot(false);el.textContent=JSON.stringify({schema:snap.schema,system:snap.system,character:snap.character,derived:snap.derived,roll_contract:snap.roll_contract},null,2)}
function updateAll(){try{auto();applyFrames();syncFrameEditor();renderProgress();renderCurrency();renderLedgers();renderPortal();checkProgression(false)}catch(e){console.error('Hyper sheet update:',e)}}
function bindAutomation(){qa('[data-currency-key]').forEach(el=>el.addEventListener('change',e=>{const w=wallet(),k=e.target.dataset.currencyKey;w[k]=number(e.target.value);schedule();renderCurrency();runAudit()}));$('hyperCurrencyUnit')?.addEventListener('change',e=>{auto().currency.unit=e.target.value||'unit';schedule();renderCurrency()});$('hyperCurrencyAdd').addEventListener('click',()=>currencyTransaction(1));$('hyperCurrencySpend').addEventListener('click',()=>currencyTransaction(-1));$('hyperCurrencyNormalize').addEventListener('click',normalizeCurrency);$('hyperAwardProgress').addEventListener('click',recordProgress);$('hyperApplyProgress').addEventListener('click',()=>openModal('Apply Progression',advancementChecklist(),'',true));$('hyperProgressTarget')?.addEventListener('change',()=>{renderProgress();checkProgression(false)});$('hyperCopyPortalData').addEventListener('click',async()=>{const text=JSON.stringify(portalSnapshot(),null,2);try{await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);$('hyperCopyPortalData').textContent='Copied';setTimeout(()=>$('hyperCopyPortalData').textContent='Copy Portal Snapshot',1200)}catch(_){openModal('Portal Snapshot',`<div class="portal-contract">${html(text)}</div>`,'',true)}});$('hyperTogglePortalMode').addEventListener('click',()=>setPortalMode(!auto().portal.enabled));document.addEventListener('input',e=>{if(e.target.closest('#studio-automation,#frameEditor'))return;clearTimeout(window.__hyperUpdateTimer);window.__hyperUpdateTimer=setTimeout(updateAll,60)},true);document.addEventListener('change',e=>{if(e.target.closest('#studio-automation,#frameEditor'))return;setTimeout(updateAll,0)},true)}
function currentRollContract(){const s=stateRef();switch(CFG.id){case'fate-core':{const skill=s.skills?.find(x=>x.name===s.roll?.skill);return{kind:'fate_action',dice:'4dF',skill:s.roll?.skill,skill_rating:number(skill?.rating),modifier:number(s.roll?.modifier),invokes:number(s.roll?.invokeCount),opposition:number(s.roll?.opposition),action:s.roll?.action}}case'gurps-4e':return{kind:'gurps_success_roll',dice:'3d6',target:number(s.combat?.attackSkill)+number(s.combat?.attackModifier),roll_under:true};case'coc-7e':return{kind:'coc_percentile',dice:'d100',skill:s.roll?.skill,target:number(s.roll?.target),difficulty:s.roll?.difficulty,bonus_penalty:number(s.roll?.bonusPenalty)};case'daggerheart':return{kind:'daggerheart_action',dice:'2d12_hope_fear',trait:s.roll?.trait,trait_modifier:number(s.traits?.[s.roll?.trait]),modifier:number(s.roll?.modifier),advantage:number(s.roll?.advantage),difficulty:number(s.roll?.difficulty),experience_index:number(s.roll?.experienceIndex)};case'pathfinder-2e-remastered':return{kind:'pf2e_check',dice:'d20',type:s.roll?.type,key:s.roll?.key,dc:number(s.roll?.dc),modifier:number(s.roll?.modifier),map:number(s.roll?.map),agile:!!s.roll?.agile};case'pbta-adapter':{const move=s.moves?.[number(s.roll?.moveIndex)-1];const stat=s.stats?.find(x=>x.name===move?.stat);return{kind:'pbta_move',dice:s.adapter?.resolution,move:move?.name,stat:move?.stat,stat_value:number(stat?.value),modifier:number(s.roll?.modifier)+number(s.roll?.forward)+number(s.roll?.ongoing),bands:copy(s.adapter)}}case'swade':return{kind:'swade_trait',dice:`d${s.roll?.traitType==='attribute'?number(s.attributes?.[s.roll?.trait]):number(s.skills?.find(x=>x.name===s.roll?.trait)?.die)}${s.roll?.wildCard?'+wild d6':''}`,trait:s.roll?.trait,modifier:number(s.roll?.modifier)-(s.roll?.woundPenalty?number(s.resources?.wounds):0),target:number(s.roll?.target),acing:true};case'blades-in-the-dark':{const action=s.actions?.find(x=>x.name===s.roll?.action),pool=number(action?.rating)+number(s.roll?.bonus)+(s.roll?.push?1:0)+(s.roll?.assist?1:0)+(s.roll?.devilsBargain?1:0);return{kind:'bitd_action',dice_pool:pool,zero_dice:pool<=0,action:s.roll?.action,position:s.roll?.position,effect:s.roll?.effect}}case'dnd-5e-5.5e':return{kind:'dnd_d20_test',dice:'d20',level:totalLevel(),proficiency_bonus:2+Math.floor(Math.max(0,totalLevel()-1)/4),note:'Request a specific skill, save, initiative, attack, or spell action from the exported character state.'};default:return{kind:'custom'}}}
function resolveExternalRoll(payload={}){const c=currentRollContract(),s=stateRef();let out={system:CFG.id,kind:c.kind,received:copy(payload),at:now()};if(c.kind==='fate_action'){const dice=Array.isArray(payload.dice)?payload.dice.reduce((n,x)=>n+number(x),0):number(payload.diceTotal);const total=dice+c.skill_rating+c.modifier+c.invokes*2,shifts=total-c.opposition;out={...out,total,shifts,outcome:shifts<0?'Fail':shifts===0?'Tie':shifts<3?'Succeed':'Succeed with style'}}else if(c.kind==='gurps_success_roll'){const roll=number(payload.total),margin=c.target-roll;out={...out,total:roll,target:c.target,margin,outcome:roll<=c.target?'Success':'Failure'}}else if(c.kind==='coc_percentile'){const roll=number(payload.total),target=c.target,hard=Math.floor(target/2),extreme=Math.floor(target/5);let level=roll===1?'Critical':roll<=extreme?'Extreme':roll<=hard?'Hard':roll<=target?'Regular':roll>=96?'Fumble':'Failure';const needed=c.difficulty==='Extreme'?'Extreme':c.difficulty==='Hard'?'Hard':'Regular',rank={Failure:0,Fumble:0,Regular:1,Hard:2,Extreme:3,Critical:4};out={...out,total:roll,target,success:rank[level]>=rank[needed],level,needed}}else if(c.kind==='daggerheart_action'){const hope=number(payload.hope),fear=number(payload.fear),exp=c.experience_index>=0?number(s.experiences?.[c.experience_index]?.value):0,total=hope+fear+c.trait_modifier+c.modifier+exp+number(payload.advantageTotal),critical=hope===fear,duality=hope>=fear?'Hope':'Fear';out={...out,hope,fear,total,critical,duality,success:critical||total>=c.difficulty}}else if(c.kind==='pf2e_check'){const die=number(payload.die),baseMod=number(payload.totalModifier??c.modifier),total=die+baseMod,delta=total-c.dc;let degree=delta>=10?3:delta>=0?2:delta<=-10?0:1;if(die===20)degree=Math.min(3,degree+1);if(die===1)degree=Math.max(0,degree-1);out={...out,die,total,dc:c.dc,degree:['Critical Failure','Failure','Success','Critical Success'][degree]}}else if(c.kind==='pbta_move'){const dice=number(payload.total),total=dice+c.modifier,a=c.bands;let band=total>=number(a.advancedMin)?a.advancedLabel:total>=number(a.highMin)?a.hitLabel:total<=number(a.lowMax)?a.missLabel:a.mixedLabel;out={...out,dice,total,band}}else if(c.kind==='swade_trait'){const trait=number(payload.traitTotal),wild=payload.wildTotal===undefined?null:number(payload.wildTotal),best=wild===null?trait:Math.max(trait,wild),total=best+c.modifier,raises=total>=c.target?Math.floor((total-c.target)/4):0;out={...out,trait,wild,total,target:c.target,success:total>=c.target,raises}}else if(c.kind==='bitd_action'){const dice=Array.isArray(payload.dice)?payload.dice.map(number):[],sorted=[...dice].sort((a,b)=>b-a),highest=sorted[0]||0,critical=sorted.filter(x=>x===6).length>=2&&!c.zero_dice;out={...out,dice,highest,critical,outcome:critical?'Critical':highest===6?'Full success':highest>=4?'Partial success':'Bad outcome'}}else if(c.kind==='dnd_d20_test'){const die=number(payload.die),modifier=number(payload.modifier),total=die+modifier;out={...out,die,modifier,total,dc:payload.dc===undefined?null:number(payload.dc),success:payload.dc===undefined?null:total>=number(payload.dc)}}auto().portal.lastResult=out;auto().portal.lastRequest=c;schedule();renderPortal();window.parent!==window&&window.parent.postMessage({type:'TABLEGATE_ROLL_RESOLVED',payload:out},'*');return out}
function portalSnapshot(full=true){const s=stateRef();return{schema:'tablegate.character-sheet.v1',system:{id:CFG.id,name:CFG.name},character:{name:characterName(),profile:copy(profile())},derived:copy(s.computed?.outputs||{}),resources:copy(s.resources||s.hp||{}),progression:copy(auto().progression),currency:{summary:currencyTotal(),wallet:copy(Object.fromEntries(qa('[data-currency-key]').map(el=>[el.dataset.currencyKey,number(wallet()[el.dataset.currencyKey])]))),history:full?copy(auto().currency.history):undefined},roll_contract:currentRollContract(),state:full?copy(s):undefined,capabilities:{generates_dice:false,accepts_external_roll_results:true,automatic_math:true,progression_popups:true,currency_math:true,circle_crop_art:true,border_textures:true}}}
function notifyChange(){clearTimeout(window.__hyperNotifyTimer);window.__hyperNotifyTimer=setTimeout(()=>{const message={type:'TABLEGATE_SHEET_STATE_CHANGED',payload:portalSnapshot(false)};try{window.parent!==window&&window.parent.postMessage(message,'*')}catch(_){}window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('tablegate-sheet-change',{detail:message.payload}))},80)}
function setPortalMode(enabled){auto().portal.enabled=!!enabled;document.body.classList.toggle('portal-mode',!!enabled);schedule();renderPortal();return auto().portal.enabled}
function bindMessages(){window.addEventListener('message',e=>{const m=e.data;if(!m||typeof m!=='object'||!String(m.type||'').startsWith('TABLEGATE_'))return;auto().portal.connected=true;if(m.type==='TABLEGATE_GET_SHEET_STATE')e.source?.postMessage({type:'TABLEGATE_SHEET_STATE',requestId:m.requestId,payload:portalSnapshot()},e.origin==='null'?'*':e.origin);else if(m.type==='TABLEGATE_GET_ROLL_REQUEST')e.source?.postMessage({type:'TABLEGATE_ROLL_REQUEST',requestId:m.requestId,payload:currentRollContract()},e.origin==='null'?'*':e.origin);else if(m.type==='TABLEGATE_APPLY_ROLL_RESULT')resolveExternalRoll(m.payload||{});else if(m.type==='TABLEGATE_SET_PORTAL_MODE')setPortalMode(m.enabled!==false);else if(m.type==='TABLEGATE_IMPORT_CHARACTER'&&m.payload){try{if(typeof importObject==='function')importObject(m.payload);else if(typeof loadImported==='function')loadImported(m.payload);updateAll()}catch(err){e.source?.postMessage({type:'TABLEGATE_ERROR',message:String(err)},'*')}}});try{window.parent!==window&&window.parent.postMessage({type:'TABLEGATE_SHEET_READY',payload:portalSnapshot(false)},'*')}catch(_){} }
function wrapRender(){try{if(typeof renderAll==='function'&&!renderAll.__hyperWrapped){const old=renderAll;renderAll=function(){const r=old.apply(this,arguments);setTimeout(updateAll,0);return r};renderAll.__hyperWrapped=true}else if(typeof render==='function'&&!render.__hyperWrapped){const old=render;render=function(){const r=old.apply(this,arguments);setTimeout(updateAll,0);return r};render.__hyperWrapped=true}}catch(_){} }
function init(){auto();['frameEditor','studio-automation','hyperProgressModal'].forEach(id=>document.getElementById(id)?.remove());buildFrameEditor();applyFrames();buildAutomationModule();wrapRender();bindMessages();setPortalMode(auto().portal.enabled);updateAll();window.TableGateCharacterSheet={version:'1.0.0',system:CFG,getSnapshot:portalSnapshot,getRollRequest:currentRollContract,applyRollResult:resolveExternalRoll,setPortalMode,subscribe(fn){const h=e=>fn(e.detail);window.addEventListener('tablegate-sheet-change',h);return()=>window.removeEventListener('tablegate-sheet-change',h)},recalculate:updateAll};window.__HYPER_SHEET_TEST_API__={config:CFG,art:()=>copy(auto().artwork),progression:progressionInfo,currency:currencyTotal,audit:runAudit,snapshot:()=>portalSnapshot(false),resolve:resolveExternalRoll,modal:()=>$('hyperProgressModal')?.classList.contains('open')}}
if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>setTimeout(init,0),{once:true});else setTimeout(init,0);
})();
