let libraryCache=null;
function textFromObject(o){const keys=['description','desc','text','effect','rules','summary','trigger','on_hit','success','failure','cost','range','damage','duration','requirements','prerequisites','notes'];let parts=[];for(const k of keys){const v=o?.[k];if(typeof v==='string'&&v.trim())parts.push(v.trim());else if(Array.isArray(v)&&v.length&&v.every(x=>typeof x==='string'))parts.push(v.join('; '))}return parts.join('\n').slice(0,5000)}
function detectType(title,text=''){const s=(title+' '+text).toLowerCase();if(/spell|cantrip|ritual|domain card/.test(s))return'Spell / power';if(/attack|weapon|strike|damage/.test(s))return'Attack';if(/move|action/.test(s))return'Move / action';if(/feat|edge|stunt|feature|ability|advantage|talent|perk/.test(s))return'Feature / ability';if(/skill|trait|attribute/.test(s))return'Skill / trait';return'Rule / option'}
function buildLibrary(){if(libraryCache)return libraryCache;const items=[],seen=new Set();const add=(title,text,source='Character sheet')=>{title=String(title||'').trim();text=String(text||'').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();if(!title||title.length>180||(!text&&source!=='Character state'))return;const key=(title+'|'+text.slice(0,180)).toLowerCase();if(seen.has(key))return;seen.add(key);items.push({title,text:text||'No description has been entered yet.',source,type:detectType(title,text)})};
 const selectors=['.attack-card','.spell-card','.feature-card','.feat-card','.stunt-card','.power-card','.move-card','.ability-card','.edge-card','[data-card-index]','.repeat-row'];for(const sel of selectors)$$(sel).forEach(el=>{const title=el.querySelector('h3,h4,b,label,input')?.textContent?.trim()||el.querySelector('input')?.value||'';const text=el.innerText||el.textContent||'';if(text.length>15)add(title,text,'Current character')});
 const s=sheetState();for(const key of ['attacks','weapons','spells','features','feats','stunts','powers','moves','abilities','edges','advantages','skills','traits','domainCards','inventory'])if(Array.isArray(s[key]))s[key].forEach(o=>{if(o&&typeof o==='object')add(o.name||o.title||o.label||key,textFromObject(o)||JSON.stringify(o,null,2).slice(0,3000),'Character state')});
 try{const raw=$('#rulesReference')?.textContent;if(raw){const data=JSON.parse(raw),visited=new WeakSet();let count=0;const walk=(v,depth=0)=>{if(!v||typeof v!=='object'||depth>12||count>2200||visited.has(v))return;visited.add(v);if(!Array.isArray(v)){const title=v.name||v.title||v.label||v.heading;const text=textFromObject(v);if(title&&text){add(title,text,'Embedded rules reference');count++}}if(Array.isArray(v))for(const x of v)walk(x,depth+1);else for(const x of Object.values(v))walk(x,depth+1)};walk(data)}}catch(_){ }
 libraryCache=items.slice(0,2600);return libraryCache}
function libraryView(){const items=buildLibrary();return `<div class="assist-status">Search the character’s selected options and the embedded system reference. Open an entry for its full description, roll procedure, roleplay guidance, and portal preparation.</div><div class="assist-library-controls"><input id="assistLibrarySearch" type="search" placeholder="Search an ability, attack, spell, feat, move, power, skill, or rule"><select id="assistLibraryType"><option value="">All types</option>${[...new Set(items.map(x=>x.type))].sort().map(x=>`<option>${esc(x)}</option>`).join('')}</select></div><div class="assist-library-list" id="assistLibraryList"></div><div class="assist-status" id="assistLibraryCount"></div>`}
function renderLibraryResults(){const items=buildLibrary(),q=($('#assistLibrarySearch')?.value||'').trim().toLowerCase(),type=$('#assistLibraryType')?.value||'';const matches=items.filter(x=>(!type||x.type===type)&&(!q||(x.title+' '+x.text+' '+x.source).toLowerCase().includes(q))).slice(0,80);$('#assistLibraryList').innerHTML=matches.map(x=>`<button class="assist-library-item" type="button" data-library-key="${esc(items.indexOf(x))}"><b>${esc(x.title)}</b><span>${esc(x.type)} · ${esc(x.source)}</span><span>${esc(x.text.slice(0,220))}</span></button>`).join('')||'<div class="assist-warning">No matching entry found. Try a shorter term or add the option to the character sheet.</div>';$('#assistLibraryCount').textContent=`Showing ${matches.length} of ${items.length} indexed entries.`;$$('[data-library-key]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>libraryDetail(items[num(b.dataset.libraryKey)]))}
function itemGuidance(item){const type=item.type;let use=GUIDE.roll;if(type==='Spell / power')use+=' Confirm casting/action cost, target, range, components, duration, concentration/sustain, save or attack procedure, damage/effect, and expended slot or resource.';else if(type==='Attack')use+=' Confirm target, reach/range, attack pool or modifier, defense/AC, damage expression, armor, critical or raise rules, ammunition, and action cost.';else if(type==='Feature / ability')use+=' Confirm its trigger, prerequisites, frequency, resource cost, action/reaction timing, targets, duration, and whether it modifies another roll.';return use}
function libraryDetail(item){if(!item)return;$('#unifiedAssistTitle').textContent=item.title;$('#unifiedAssistBody').innerHTML=`${tabs('library')}<div class="assist-card"><h3>${esc(item.title)}</h3><div class="assist-status">${esc(item.type)} · ${esc(item.source)}</div><p class="assist-detail">${esc(item.text)}</p></div><div class="assist-card"><h3>How to roll or resolve it</h3><p>${esc(itemGuidance(item))}</p><h3>How to roleplay it</h3><p>${esc(GUIDE.roleplay)}</p><h3>What the sheet tracks afterward</h3><p>Apply every resulting resource, damage, stress, harm, condition, slot, use, ammo, currency, XP, relationship, faction, clock, or narrative consequence in the appropriate tracker. The portal supplies dice results; the sheet owns the math and state.</p></div><div class="assist-actions"><button id="assistUseLibraryItem" type="button">Prepare Related Portal Roll</button><button id="assistBackLibrary" class="secondary" type="button">Back to Library</button></div>`;$$('[data-assist-tab]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>open(b.dataset.assistTab));$('#assistUseLibraryItem').onclick=()=>open('roll',{label:item.title,notes:item.text+'\n\n'+itemGuidance(item)});$('#assistBackLibrary').onclick=()=>render('library')}
function levelChecklist(){const modal=$('#unifiedAssistModal');modal.dataset.tab='progress';$('#unifiedAssistTitle').textContent='Full Level-Up / Advancement Checklist';$('#unifiedAssistBody').innerHTML=`${tabs('progress')}<div class="assist-card"><h3>${esc(CFG.name)}</h3><p>${esc(GUIDE.level)}</p><ol><li>Confirm the exact trigger: XP threshold, milestone, development phase, awarded character points, filled track, or Guide decision.</li><li>Record the advancement in the progression history before changing the character.</li><li>Apply level, rank, tier, Advance, milestone, or point expenditure and preserve excess progress where the system requires it.</li><li>Recalculate every derived value, defense, target, pool, proficiency, threshold, capacity, passive value, movement value, and carrying limit.</li><li>Choose all required and optional abilities, feats, spells, powers, moves, stunts, Edges, upgrades, skill changes, attribute changes, or domain/playbook/class options.</li><li>Validate prerequisites, caps, duplicates, point budgets, legal selections, known/prepared limits, and multiclass or archetype requirements.</li><li>Update HP, Stress, Sanity, Harm, armor, spell slots, power points, Hope/Fear, Bennies, Fate points, load, ammo, consumables, recovery, and limited-use resources.</li><li>Review attacks, damage, DCs, action costs, reactions, assistance, conditions, range, targets, areas, durations, concentration/sustain, and equipment.</li><li>Prompt for a new goal, relationship change, faction consequence, visual change, title, reputation, bond, belief, drive, aspect, or other narrative growth.</li><li>Save and export the interactive HTML. The future portal account can store the updated sheet and use its new roll contract.</li></ol></div><div class="assist-actions"><button id="assistBackProgress" class="secondary" type="button">Back to Progression</button></div>`;$$('[data-assist-tab]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>open(b.dataset.assistTab));$('#assistBackProgress').onclick=()=>render('progress')}
function syncRelationshipRows(){const d=store();$$('[data-rel-row]').forEach(row=>{const i=num(row.dataset.relRow),r=d.relationships[i]||(d.relationships[i]={});$$('[data-rel]',row).forEach(el=>r[el.dataset.rel]=el.value)})}
function syncFactionRows(){const d=store();$$('[data-faction-row]').forEach(row=>{const i=num(row.dataset.factionRow),f=d.factions[i]||(d.factions[i]={});$$('[data-faction]',row).forEach(el=>f[el.dataset.faction]=el.value)})}
function bind(tab,ctx){$$('[data-assist-tab]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>open(b.dataset.assistTab));
 if(tab==='roll'){$('#assistSendRoll').onclick=()=>{const payload={...currentContract(),label:$('#assistRollLabel').value,mode:$('#assistRollMode').value,extra_modifier:num($('#assistRollModifier').value),target:$('#assistRollTarget').value,notes:$('#assistRollNotes').value,character:clone(sheetState())};post('TABLEGATE_ROLL_REQUEST',payload);post('tablegate.character-sheet.roll-request',payload);toast('Roll request sent to the parent portal.');close()};$('#assistCopyRoll').onclick=()=>{const payload={...currentContract(),label:$('#assistRollLabel').value,mode:$('#assistRollMode').value,extra_modifier:num($('#assistRollModifier').value),target:$('#assistRollTarget').value,notes:$('#assistRollNotes').value};navigator.clipboard?.writeText(JSON.stringify(payload,null,2)).then(()=>toast('Roll request copied.'))}}
 if(tab==='checks')$$('[data-quick-roll]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>open('roll',{label:b.dataset.quickRoll}));
 if(tab==='identity'){$('#saveIdentity').onclick=()=>{const d=store().identity;for(const [id,key] of [['idPlayerName','playerName'],['idPlayerPronouns','playerPronouns'],['idPlayerPronounsCustom','playerPronounsCustom'],['idPlayerGender','playerGender'],['idPlayerGenderCustom','playerGenderCustom'],['idCharacterName','characterName'],['idCharacterPronouns','characterPronouns'],['idCharacterPronounsCustom','characterPronounsCustom'],['idCharacterGender','characterGender'],['idCharacterGenderCustom','characterGenderCustom'],['idIdentityNotes','notes']])d[key]=$('#'+id).value;const p=profile();if(d.playerName)p.player=d.playerName;if(d.characterName){if('name'in p||!('characterName'in p))p.name=d.characterName;else p.characterName=d.characterName}const cp=d.characterPronouns==='Write-in'||d.characterPronouns==='Neopronouns'?d.characterPronounsCustom:d.characterPronouns;if(cp)p.pronouns=cp;store().events.push({type:'identity',at:new Date().toISOString(),note:'Identity profile updated'});persist();toast('Player and character identity saved.');render('identity')}}
 if(tab==='relationships'){$('#addRelationship').onclick=()=>{syncRelationshipRows();store().relationships.push({name:'',type:'Friend',status:'Positive',strength:1,notes:''});persist();render('relationships');toast('Relationship added. Complete its roleplay prompts.')};$('#saveRelationships').onclick=()=>{syncRelationshipRows();store().events.push({type:'relationships',at:new Date().toISOString(),count:store().relationships.length});persist();toast('Relationships and bond values saved.');updateSummary()};$$('[data-rel-delete]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{syncRelationshipRows();store().relationships.splice(num(b.dataset.relDelete),1);persist();render('relationships');toast('Relationship removed.')})}
 if(tab==='factions'){$('#addFaction').onclick=()=>{syncFactionRows();store().factions.push({name:'',type:'Friendly faction',standing:0,resource:'',notes:''});persist();render('factions');toast('Faction added. Add its agenda and consequences.')};$('#saveFactions').onclick=()=>{syncFactionRows();store().events.push({type:'factions',at:new Date().toISOString(),count:store().factions.length});persist();toast('Faction standings saved.');updateSummary()};$$('[data-faction-delete]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{syncFactionRows();store().factions.splice(num(b.dataset.factionDelete),1);persist();render('factions');toast('Faction removed.')})}
 if(tab==='progress'){$('#assistRecordProgress').onclick=()=>{const amount=$('#assistProgressAmount').value,memo=$('#assistProgressMemo').value;if($('#assistProgressTarget')&&$('#hyperProgressTarget'))$('#hyperProgressTarget').value=$('#assistProgressTarget').value;if($('#assistProgressTrack')&&$('#hyperProgressTrack'))$('#hyperProgressTrack').value=$('#assistProgressTrack').value;if($('#hyperProgressAmount'))$('#hyperProgressAmount').value=amount;if($('#hyperProgressMemo'))$('#hyperProgressMemo').value=memo;$('#hyperAwardProgress')?.click();setTimeout(()=>{toast(`Recorded ${amount} progression: ${memo||'no memo entered'}.`);if($('#hyperProgressModal')?.classList.contains('open'))close();else render('progress')},90)};$('#assistApplyProgress').onclick=()=>{close();$('#hyperApplyProgress')?.click();setTimeout(()=>toast('Advancement procedure opened or applied. Review every prompted choice.'),90)};$('#assistLevelChecklist').onclick=levelChecklist}
 if(tab==='currency'){$('#assistCurrencyAdd').onclick=()=>currencyAction('add');$('#assistCurrencySpend').onclick=()=>currencyAction('spend');$('#assistCurrencyNormalize').onclick=()=>{$('#hyperCurrencyNormalize')?.click();setTimeout(()=>{toast('Currency normalized using this system’s conversion rules.');render('currency')},90)}}
 if(tab==='library'){$('#assistLibrarySearch').addEventListener('input',renderLibraryResults);$('#assistLibraryType').addEventListener('change',renderLibraryResults);renderLibraryResults()}}
function currencyAction(kind){const amount=Math.max(0,num($('#assistCurrencyAmount').value)),denom=$('#assistCurrencyDenom').value,memo=$('#assistCurrencyMemo').value,current=num($(`[data-currency-key="${CSS.escape(denom)}"]`)?.value);if(kind==='spend'&&current<amount){const warn=document.createElement('div');warn.className='assist-warning';warn.textContent=`Insufficient ${denom}: the current balance is ${current}, but this transaction needs ${amount}.`;$('#unifiedAssistBody').prepend(warn);toast(`Insufficient ${denom}. Transaction not applied.`);return}if($('#hyperCurrencyAmount'))$('#hyperCurrencyAmount').value=amount;if($('#hyperCurrencyDenom'))$('#hyperCurrencyDenom').value=denom;if($('#hyperCurrencyMemo'))$('#hyperCurrencyMemo').value=memo;const before=$('#hyperCurrencyTotal')?.textContent||'';$(kind==='add'?'#hyperCurrencyAdd':'#hyperCurrencySpend')?.click();setTimeout(()=>{const after=$('#hyperCurrencyTotal')?.textContent||'';toast(`${kind==='add'?'Added':'Spent'} ${amount} ${denom}. ${before===after?'Review the tracker for any validation message.':'Ledger and totals updated.'}`);render('currency')},110)}
function updateSummary(){const d=store(),el=$('#immersiveProfileSummary');if(!el)return;const ident=d.identity||{};const cp=ident.characterPronouns==='Write-in'||ident.characterPronouns==='Neopronouns'?ident.characterPronounsCustom:ident.characterPronouns;el.innerHTML=`<div class="immersive-summary-grid"><div><b>${esc(cp||profile().pronouns||'Not set')}</b><span>Character pronouns</span></div><div><b>${d.relationships.length}</b><span>Relationships</span></div><div><b>${d.factions.length}</b><span>Factions</span></div></div>`}
function fixFullArt(){const p=$('#portraitInput,#portraitFile'),s=$('#secondInput,#secondaryArtFile');if(p?.parentElement){p.parentElement.id='fullCharacterArtOne';p.parentElement.classList.add('full-character-art-slot');const b=p.parentElement.querySelector('.art-drop-label b');if(b)b.textContent='Full Character Art 1';const span=p.parentElement.querySelector('.art-drop-label span');if(span)span.innerHTML='Full-body or primary character illustration<br>Drop an image or click to choose'}if(s?.parentElement){s.parentElement.id='fullCharacterArtTwo';s.parentElement.classList.add('full-character-art-slot');const b=s.parentElement.querySelector('.art-drop-label b');if(b)b.textContent='Full Character Art 2';const span=s.parentElement.querySelector('.art-drop-label span');if(span)span.innerHTML='Alternate outfit, pose, form, or scene art<br>Drop an image or click to choose'}$('#frameEditor')?.remove()}
function tokenData(){const s=sheetState();s.studioAutomation=s.studioAutomation||{};s.studioAutomation.artwork=s.studioAutomation.artwork||{};s.studioAutomation.artwork.token=s.studioAutomation.artwork.token||{zoom:1,x:0,y:0,borderColor:'#c9a84c',borderWidth:14,borderImage:''};return s.studioAutomation.artwork.token}
function imageToData(file,cb,max=1000){if(!file||!String(file.type).startsWith('image/'))return;const r=new FileReader();r.onload=()=>{const im=new Image();im.onload=()=>{const scale=Math.min(1,max/Math.max(im.naturalWidth,im.naturalHeight)),c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=Math.max(1,Math.round(im.naturalWidth*scale));c.height=Math.max(1,Math.round(im.naturalHeight*scale));c.getContext('2d').drawImage(im,0,0,c.width,c.height);cb(c.toDataURL(file.type==='image/png'?'image/png':'image/jpeg',.9))};im.src=r.result};r.readAsDataURL(file)}
function cssUrl(v){return v?`url(${JSON.stringify(v)})`:'none'}
function applyToken(){const slot=$('#tokenArtSlot'),img=$('#tokenArtImg'),d=tokenData(),src=profile().tokenArt||'';if(!slot)return;slot.style.setProperty('--token-border',d.borderColor||'#c9a84c');slot.style.setProperty('--token-width',Math.max(4,Math.min(36,num(d.borderWidth)))+'px');slot.style.setProperty('--token-texture',cssUrl(d.borderImage));slot.style.setProperty('--token-zoom',Math.max(1,Math.min(4,num(d.zoom)||1)));slot.style.setProperty('--token-x',Math.max(-45,Math.min(45,num(d.x)))+'%');slot.style.setProperty('--token-y',Math.max(-45,Math.min(45,num(d.y)))+'%');if(src){img.src=src;slot.classList.add('has-image')}else{img.removeAttribute('src');slot.classList.remove('has-image')}for(const [id,key] of [['tokenZoom','zoom'],['tokenX','x'],['tokenY','y'],['tokenBorderWidth','borderWidth']])if($('#'+id))$('#'+id).value=d[key];if($('#tokenBorderColor'))$('#tokenBorderColor').value=/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(d.borderColor)?d.borderColor:'#c9a84c';if($('#tokenBorderHex'))$('#tokenBorderHex').value=d.borderColor||'#c9a84c';if($('#tokenTextureStatus'))$('#tokenTextureStatus').textContent=d.borderImage?'Uploaded border texture or gradient is active.':'Solid/gradient gold ring active; upload a metal, fabric, wood, or gradient image to replace it.'}
function setToken(key,val){tokenData()[key]=val;applyToken();persist()}
function buildTokenStudio(){const body=$('#studio-artwork .module-body,#artwork .module-body,#artwork .panel-body');if(!body||$('#tokenStudio'))return;const div=document.createElement('div');div.id='tokenStudio';div.className='token-studio';div.innerHTML=`<h3>Separate Circular Token Studio</h3><p class="token-studio-copy">This third image is the token only. The two character-art windows above remain full rectangular artwork. The editable ring follows the supplied gold-ring SVG reference.</p><div class="token-preview-row"><label class="token-art-slot" id="tokenArtSlot" tabindex="0"><img id="tokenArtImg" alt="Circular character token preview"><span class="token-drop-label"><b>Character Token</b><span>Drop an image or click to choose<br>Drag to reposition after upload</span></span><input id="tokenArtInput" type="file" accept="image/*"></label></div><div class="token-controls"><label>Zoom<input id="tokenZoom" type="range" min="1" max="4" step="0.01"></label><label>Horizontal crop<input id="tokenX" type="range" min="-45" max="45" step="1"></label><label>Vertical crop<input id="tokenY" type="range" min="-45" max="45" step="1"></label><label>Border thickness<input id="tokenBorderWidth" type="range" min="4" max="36" step="1"></label><label>Border color<div class="token-color-pair"><input id="tokenBorderColor" type="color"><input id="tokenBorderHex" maxlength="9" placeholder="#C9A84C"></div></label></div><div class="token-actions"><label class="file-button" for="tokenBorderTexture">Upload Border Texture<input id="tokenBorderTexture" type="file" accept="image/*,.svg"></label><button id="tokenUseGoldRing" class="secondary" type="button">Use Gold Ring Style</button><button id="tokenRemoveTexture" class="secondary" type="button">Remove Texture</button><button id="tokenClearArt" class="danger" type="button">Clear Token</button><button id="tokenReset" class="secondary" type="button">Reset Crop & Border</button></div><div class="token-status" id="tokenTextureStatus"></div>`;body.append(div);
 $('#tokenArtInput').addEventListener('change',e=>{imageToData(e.target.files?.[0],data=>{profile().tokenArt=data;applyToken();persist();toast('Character token uploaded and saved.')});e.target.value=''});const slot=$('#tokenArtSlot');['dragenter','dragover'].forEach(ev=>slot.addEventListener(ev,e=>{e.preventDefault();slot.classList.add('dragging')}));['dragleave','drop'].forEach(ev=>slot.addEventListener(ev,e=>{e.preventDefault();slot.classList.remove('dragging')}));slot.addEventListener('drop',e=>imageToData(e.dataTransfer?.files?.[0],data=>{profile().tokenArt=data;applyToken();persist();toast('Character token dropped into the circular crop.')}));
 for(const [id,key] of [['tokenZoom','zoom'],['tokenX','x'],['tokenY','y'],['tokenBorderWidth','borderWidth']])$('#'+id).addEventListener('input',e=>setToken(key,num(e.target.value)));$('#tokenBorderColor').addEventListener('input',e=>setToken('borderColor',e.target.value));$('#tokenBorderHex').addEventListener('change',e=>{let v=e.target.value.trim();if(!v.startsWith('#'))v='#'+v;if(/^#[0-9a-f]{6}([0-9a-f]{2})?$/i.test(v))setToken('borderColor',v);else applyToken()});$('#tokenBorderTexture').addEventListener('change',e=>{imageToData(e.target.files?.[0],data=>{setToken('borderImage',data);toast('Token border texture uploaded.')},1200);e.target.value=''});$('#tokenUseGoldRing').onclick=()=>{setToken('borderImage','');setToken('borderColor','#c9a84c');setToken('borderWidth',14);toast('Gold-ring reference style restored.')};$('#tokenRemoveTexture').onclick=()=>{setToken('borderImage','');toast('Token border texture removed.')};$('#tokenClearArt').onclick=()=>{profile().tokenArt='';applyToken();persist();toast('Token image cleared; the two full character-art images were not changed.')};$('#tokenReset').onclick=()=>{Object.assign(tokenData(),{zoom:1,x:0,y:0,borderColor:'#c9a84c',borderWidth:14,borderImage:''});applyToken();persist();toast('Token crop and border reset.')};
 let start=null,moved=false;slot.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{if(!profile().tokenArt||e.button>0)return;const d=tokenData();start={x:e.clientX,y:e.clientY,fx:num(d.x),fy:num(d.y)};moved=false;slot.setPointerCapture?.(e.pointerId)});slot.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{if(!start)return;const dx=e.clientX-start.x,dy=e.clientY-start.y;if(Math.hypot(dx,dy)>4)moved=true;if(!moved)return;tokenData().x=Math.max(-45,Math.min(45,start.fx+dx/3));tokenData().y=Math.max(-45,Math.min(45,start.fy+dy/3));slot.classList.add('dragging');applyToken()});slot.addEventListener('pointerup',e=>{if(!start)return;slot.classList.remove('dragging');try{slot.releasePointerCapture?.(e.pointerId)}catch(_){}if(moved){e.preventDefault();persist();slot.dataset.suppressClick='1';setTimeout(()=>delete slot.dataset.suppressClick,20)}start=null});slot.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(slot.dataset.suppressClick){e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation()}},true);applyToken()}
function buildImmersiveSummary(){const identity=$('#identity');if(!identity||$('#immersionProfileModule'))return;const sec=document.createElement('section');sec.className='module';sec.id='immersionProfileModule';sec.innerHTML=`<div class="module-hd"><h2>Identity, Relationships & Factions</h2><span class="tiny" style="color:#fff">Popup-guided and portal-ready</span></div><div class="module-body"><div class="immersive-summary" id="immersiveProfileSummary"></div><p class="help">Track the player separately from the character, use inclusive pronoun and gender lists or write-ins, and record relationship and faction consequences that shape roleplay.</p><div class="assist-actions"><button type="button" data-open-assist="identity">Identity & Pronouns</button><button type="button" data-open-assist="relationships" class="secondary">Relationships</button><button type="button" data-open-assist="factions" class="secondary">Factions</button></div></div>`;identity.insertAdjacentElement('afterend',sec);$$('[data-open-assist]',sec).forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>open(b.dataset.openAssist));updateSummary()}
function bindTop(){$$('[data-open-assist]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>open(b.dataset.openAssist));$('[data-assist-close]')?.addEventListener('click',close);$('#unifiedAssistModal')?.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target.id==='unifiedAssistModal')close()});document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Escape'&&!$('#unifiedAssistModal')?.hidden)close()})}
function interceptRolls(){const actions=new Set(Object.keys(CFG.roll||{}));document.addEventListener('click',e=>{const b=e.target.closest('[data-action]');if(!b||!actions.has(b.dataset.action))return;e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();open('roll',{label:b.textContent.trim(),notes:`Prepared from the ${CFG.name} action “${b.dataset.action}”. ${CFG.roll[b.dataset.action]||''}`})},true)}

(()=>{
'use strict';
function initTrueMenu(){
  const bubble=document.getElementById('characterSheetBubble');
  const toggle=document.getElementById('characterSheetBubbleToggle');
  const panel=document.getElementById('characterSheetBubblePanel');
  if(!bubble||!toggle||!panel||bubble.dataset.trueMenuReady==='1')return;
  bubble.dataset.trueMenuReady='1';
  toggle.title='Open navigation and sheet design menu. Drag the button to move it.';
  panel.setAttribute('role','dialog');
  panel.setAttribute('aria-modal','true');
  panel.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
  panel.setAttribute('tabindex','-1');

  const backdrop=document.createElement('button');
  backdrop.type='button';
  backdrop.className='cs-menu-backdrop';
  backdrop.setAttribute('aria-label','Close menu');
  bubble.insertBefore(backdrop,panel);

  const title=panel.querySelector('.cs-panel-title');
  const header=document.createElement('div');
  header.className='cs-menu-header';
  const close=document.createElement('button');
  close.type='button';
  close.className='cs-menu-close';
  close.setAttribute('aria-label','Close menu');
  close.textContent='×';
  panel.insertBefore(header,panel.firstChild);
  if(title)header.appendChild(title);
  header.appendChild(close);

  const navGroup=document.createElement('div');
  navGroup.className='cs-menu-nav';
  navGroup.innerHTML='<span class="cs-label">Quick navigation</span><div class="cs-menu-link-grid"></div>';
  header.insertAdjacentElement('afterend',navGroup);
  const grid=navGroup.querySelector('.cs-menu-link-grid');
  const navLinks=[...document.querySelectorAll('nav.nav a[href^="#"]')];
  const targets=[{label:'Top of Sheet',target:'__top'},...navLinks.map(a=>({label:(a.textContent||'Section').trim(),target:a.getAttribute('href').slice(1)})),{label:'Bottom of Sheet',target:'__bottom'}];
  const seen=new Set();
  targets.forEach(item=>{
    const key=item.target+'|'+item.label;
    if(seen.has(key))return;
    seen.add(key);
    const button=document.createElement('button');
    button.type='button';
    button.className='cs-menu-link';
    button.textContent=item.label;
    button.dataset.jumpTarget=item.target;
    grid.appendChild(button);
  });

  function closeMenu(returnFocus=false){
    bubble.classList.remove('open');
    toggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');
    if(returnFocus)toggle.focus({preventScroll:true});
  }
  close.addEventListener('click',()=>closeMenu(true));
  backdrop.addEventListener('click',()=>closeMenu(true));
  grid.addEventListener('click',event=>{
    const button=event.target.closest('.cs-menu-link');
    if(!button)return;
    const target=button.dataset.jumpTarget;
    if(target==='__top')window.scrollTo({top:0,behavior:'smooth'});
    else if(target==='__bottom')window.scrollTo({top:document.documentElement.scrollHeight,behavior:'smooth'});
    else document.getElementById(target)?.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth',block:'start'});
    closeMenu(false);
  });

  let wasOpen=false;
  function syncOpenState(){
    const open=bubble.classList.contains('open');
    document.body.classList.toggle('cs-menu-open',open);
    panel.setAttribute('aria-hidden',String(!open));
    if(open!==wasOpen){
      if(open)requestAnimationFrame(()=>close.focus({preventScroll:true}));
      else if(panel.contains(document.activeElement))toggle.focus({preventScroll:true});
      wasOpen=open;
    }
  }
  new MutationObserver(syncOpenState).observe(bubble,{attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class']});
  syncOpenState();
}
if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',initTrueMenu,{once:true});else initTrueMenu();
})();
</script>

const STORE='ttrpg-rules-homebrew-engine:'+SYSTEM_ID+':v2';
const $=id=>document.getElementById(id); const esc=s=>String(s??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const uid=()=>Math.random().toString(36).slice(2)+Date.now().toString(36);
let state={version:2,system:SYSTEM_ID,selected:[],bases:[{id:uid(),key:'level',label:'Level / Rank / Tier',value:1}],formulas:[],links:[]};
function cleanKey(s){return String(s||'').trim().toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,'_').replace(/^_|_$/g,'')}
function save(){try{localStorage.setItem(STORE,JSON.stringify(state));status('Saved locally.','good')}catch(e){status('Could not save: '+e.message,'danger')}}
function load(){try{const raw=localStorage.getItem(STORE);if(raw){const x=JSON.parse(raw);if(x&&x.system===SYSTEM_ID)state=Object.assign(state,x)}}catch(e){status('Autosave could not be loaded.','danger')}}
function status(msg,type=''){const n=$('rheStatus');if(!n)return;n.textContent=msg;n.className='rhe-status '+(type==='good'?'rhe-good':type==='danger'?'rhe-danger':'');}
function download(name,obj){const b=new Blob([JSON.stringify(obj,null,2)],{type:'application/json'}),u=URL.createObjectURL(b),a=document.createElement('a');a.href=u;a.download=name;a.click();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(u),1000)}
function detectFields(){const out=[];document.querySelectorAll('input,select,textarea').forEach((el,i)=>{if(el.closest('#rules-homebrew-engine')||['button','file','hidden','checkbox','radio'].includes(el.type))return;let label='';if(el.id){const l=document.querySelector(`label[for="${CSS.escape(el.id)}"]`);if(l)label=l.textContent.trim()}if(!label){const l=el.closest('label');if(l)label=l.textContent.replace(el.value||'','').trim()}if(!label)label=el.getAttribute('aria-label')||el.placeholder||el.name||el.id||`Field ${i+1}`;out.push({token:el.id?'#'+el.id:'@'+i,label:label.slice(0,90),el})});return out}
function tokenize(expr){const s=String(expr||'').trim();if(!s)return[];const re=/\s*([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*|\d*\.\d+|\d+|[()+\-*/%,])\s*/gy;let m,pos=0,t=[];while((m=re.exec(s))){if(m.index!==pos)throw Error('Unexpected token near '+s.slice(pos,pos+12));t.push(m[1]);pos=re.lastIndex}if(pos!==s.length)throw Error('Unexpected token near '+s.slice(pos,pos+12));return t}
function evalExpr(expr,ctx){const t=tokenize(expr);let p=0;const peek=()=>t[p],take=()=>t[p++];function primary(){let x=take();if(x===undefined)throw Error('Incomplete formula');if(x==='('){let v=add();if(take()!==')')throw Error('Missing )');return v}if(x==='+'||x==='-'){let v=primary();return x==='-'?-v:v}if(/^\d/.test(x))return Number(x);if(/^[A-Za-z_]/.test(x)){if(peek()==='('){take();let a=[];if(peek()!==')'){while(true){a.push(add());if(peek()===','){take();continue}break}}if(take()!==')')throw Error('Missing ) after function');const f={min:Math.min,max:Math.max,floor:Math.floor,ceil:Math.ceil,round:Math.round,abs:Math.abs}[x.toLowerCase()];if(!f)throw Error('Unknown function '+x);return f(...a)}return Number(ctx[cleanKey(x)]??0)}throw Error('Unexpected '+x)}function mul(){let v=primary();while(['*','/','%'].includes(peek())){let o=take(),r=primary();v=o==='*'?v*r:o==='/'?(r===0?0:v/r):v%r}return v}function add(){let v=mul();while(['+','-'].includes(peek())){let o=take(),r=mul();v=o==='+'?v+r:v-r}return v}const v=add();if(p<t.length)throw Error('Unexpected '+peek());return Number.isFinite(v)?v:0}
function calculate(){const ctx={},detail={};state.bases.forEach(b=>{const k=cleanKey(b.key||b.label);if(!k)return;const v=Number(b.value)||0;ctx[k]=v;detail[k]={label:b.label||k,base:v,adds:[],mults:[],sets:[],mins:[],maxs:[],final:v}});const active=state.selected.filter(o=>o.active!==false);for(const o of active){for(const ef of o.effects||[]){const k=cleanKey(ef.target);if(!k)continue;if(!detail[k])detail[k]={label:ef.target||k,base:Number(ctx[k])||0,adds:[],mults:[],sets:[],mins:[],maxs:[],final:Number(ctx[k])||0};let v=0;try{v=evalExpr(ef.value,ctx)}catch(err){v=0;ef.error=err.message}const note={value:v,from:o.name,formula:ef.value};({add:'adds',multiply:'mults',set:'sets',min:'mins',max:'maxs'}[ef.op]&&detail[k][{add:'adds',multiply:'mults',set:'sets',min:'mins',max:'maxs'}[ef.op]].push(note))}}
for(const [k,d] of Object.entries(detail)){let v=d.base;d.adds.forEach(x=>v+=x.value);d.mults.forEach(x=>v*=x.value);d.sets.forEach(x=>v=x.value);d.mins.forEach(x=>v=Math.max(v,x.value));d.maxs.forEach(x=>v=Math.min(v,x.value));d.final=Number.isFinite(v)?v:0;ctx[k]=d.final}
// derived formulas: multiple passes for dependencies
for(let pass=0;pass<Math.max(2,state.formulas.length+1);pass++)for(const f of state.formulas){const k=cleanKey(f.key||f.label);if(!k)continue;try{const v=evalExpr(f.formula,ctx);ctx[k]=v;detail[k]={label:f.label||k,base:null,adds:[],mults:[],sets:[],mins:[],maxs:[],formula:f.formula,final:v}}catch(err){detail[k]={label:f.label||k,formula:f.formula,error:err.message,final:0};ctx[k]=0}}
return{ctx,detail}}
function renderCatalog(){const q=$('rheSearch').value.trim().toLowerCase(),cat=$('rheCategory').value,kind=$('rheKind').value;let a=CATALOG.filter(x=>(!cat||x.category===cat)&&(!kind||x.kind===kind)&&(!q||[x.name,x.category,x.source,x.summary,(x.tags||[]).join(' ')].join(' ').toLowerCase().includes(q)));const cap=$('rheShowAll').dataset.all==='1'?a.length:250;a=a.slice(0,cap);$('rheCatalog').innerHTML=a.length?a.map(x=>`<div class="rhe-row"><div><b>${esc(x.name)}</b><small>${esc(x.summary||'')}</small></div><div><span class="rhe-pill">${esc(x.category)}</span><span class="rhe-pill">${esc(x.kind)}</span></div><div><small>${esc(x.source||'')}</small>${x.url?`<a class="rhe-source-link" href="${esc(x.url)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Official/live source</a>`:''}</div><button type="button" data-rhe-add="${esc(x.id)}">Add</button></div>`).join(''):`<div class="rhe-empty">No matching catalog entries.</div>`;}
function renderSelected(){const root=$('rheSelected');if(!state.selected.length){root.innerHTML='<div class="rhe-empty">No options added yet.</div>';return}root.innerHTML=state.selected.map((o,i)=>`<div class="rhe-card"><div class="rhe-card-head"><div><h4>${esc(o.name)}</h4><span class="rhe-pill">${esc(o.category)}</span><span class="rhe-pill">${esc(o.approval||o.kind||'Pending')}</span><small>${esc(o.source||'')}</small></div><div><label><input type="checkbox" data-rhe-active="${i}" ${o.active!==false?'checked':''}> Active</label> <button type="button" data-rhe-effect-add="${i}">Add Effect</button> <button type="button" data-rhe-remove="${i}">Remove</button></div></div><div class="rhe-approval"><label>Level / Rank / Tier<input data-rhe-opt="level" data-i="${i}" value="${esc(o.level||'')}"></label><label>Approval<select data-rhe-opt="approval" data-i="${i}">${['Pending DM/GM/MOL review','Approved by DM','Approved by GM','Approved by MOL','Official/open option'].map(v=>`<option ${v===(o.approval||'')?'selected':''}>${v}</option>`).join('')}</select></label></div><label>Notes / prerequisites<textarea data-rhe-opt="notes" data-i="${i}">${esc(o.notes||o.summary||'')}</textarea></label><div>${(o.effects||[]).map((ef,j)=>`<div class="rhe-effect-grid"><label>Target key<input data-rhe-ef="target" data-i="${i}" data-j="${j}" value="${esc(ef.target||'')}"></label><label>Operation<select data-rhe-ef="op" data-i="${i}" data-j="${j}">${['add','multiply','set','min','max'].map(v=>`<option ${v===ef.op?'selected':''}>${v}</option>`).join('')}</select></label><label>Value / formula<input data-rhe-ef="value" data-i="${i}" data-j="${j}" value="${esc(ef.value??0)}"></label><label>Note<input data-rhe-ef="note" data-i="${i}" data-j="${j}" value="${esc(ef.note||'')}"></label><button type="button" data-rhe-effect-remove="${i}:${j}">Remove</button>${ef.error?`<small class="rhe-danger rhe-span12">${esc(ef.error)}</small>`:''}</div>`).join('')}</div></div>`).join('')}
function renderBases(){$('rheBases').innerHTML=state.bases.map((b,i)=>`<div class="rhe-base-grid"><label>Key<input data-rhe-base="key" data-i="${i}" value="${esc(b.key||'')}"></label><label>Label<input data-rhe-base="label" data-i="${i}" value="${esc(b.label||'')}"></label><label>Base value<input type="number" step="any" data-rhe-base="value" data-i="${i}" value="${esc(b.value??0)}"></label><span></span><button type="button" data-rhe-base-remove="${i}">Remove</button></div>`).join('')}
function renderFormulas(){$('rheFormulas').innerHTML=state.formulas.map((f,i)=>`<div class="rhe-formula-grid"><label>Key<input data-rhe-form="key" data-i="${i}" value="${esc(f.key||'')}"></label><label>Label<input data-rhe-form="label" data-i="${i}" value="${esc(f.label||'')}"></label><label>Formula<input data-rhe-form="formula" data-i="${i}" value="${esc(f.formula||'')}"></label><span></span><button type="button" data-rhe-form-remove="${i}">Remove</button></div>`).join('')}
function renderLinks(){const fields=detectFields();$('rheLinks').innerHTML=state.links.map((l,i)=>`<div class="rhe-field-link"><label>Calculated key<input data-rhe-link="key" data-i="${i}" value="${esc(l.key||'')}"></label><label>Sheet field<select data-rhe-link="field" data-i="${i}"><option value="">Choose detected field</option>${fields.map(f=>`<option value="${esc(f.token)}" ${f.token===l.field?'selected':''}>${esc(f.label)} (${esc(f.token)})</option>`).join('')}</select></label><button type="button" data-rhe-link-remove="${i}">Remove</button></div>`).join('')||'<div class="rhe-empty">No field links.</div>'}
function renderLedger(){const c=calculate(),rows=Object.entries(c.detail);$('rheLedger').innerHTML=rows.length?`<div style="overflow:auto"><table class="rhe-table"><thead><tr><th>Key</th><th>Base / Formula</th><th>Active effects</th><th>Final</th></tr></thead><tbody>${rows.map(([k,d])=>`<tr><td><b>${esc(d.label||k)}</b><small>${esc(k)}</small></td><td>${d.formula?`<code>${esc(d.formula)}</code>`:esc(d.base)}</td><td>${['adds','mults','sets','mins','maxs'].flatMap(type=>(d[type]||[]).map(x=>`<div><span class="rhe-pill">${type}</span> ${esc(x.value)} — ${esc(x.from)}</div>`)).join('')||'—'}${d.error?`<div class="rhe-danger">${esc(d.error)}</div>`:''}</td><td><b>${Math.round((d.final+Number.EPSILON)*10000)/10000}</b></td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table></div>`:'<div class="rhe-empty">Add base values or formulas to calculate.</div>';return c}
function render(){renderCatalog();renderSelected();renderBases();renderFormulas();renderLinks();renderLedger()}
function addCatalog(id){const x=CATALOG.find(v=>v.id===id);if(!x)return;state.selected.push({id:uid(),catalogId:x.id,name:x.name,category:x.category,source:x.source,kind:x.kind,approval:x.kind==='official'?'Official/open option':'Pending DM/GM/MOL review',level:'',notes:x.summary||'',active:true,effects:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(x.effects||[]))});save();renderSelected();renderLedger()}
function applyLinks(){const c=calculate(),fields=detectFields();let n=0;for(const l of state.links){const k=cleanKey(l.key),v=c.ctx[k];if(v===undefined)continue;const f=fields.find(x=>x.token===l.field);if(!f)continue;f.el.value=String(Math.round((v+Number.EPSILON)*10000)/10000);f.el.dispatchEvent(new Event('input',{bubbles:true}));f.el.dispatchEvent(new Event('change',{bubbles:true}));n++}status(`Applied ${n} calculated value${n===1?'':'s'} to sheet fields.`,'good')}
function bind(){const cats=[...new Set(CATALOG.map(x=>x.category))].sort();$('rheCategory').innerHTML='<option value="">All categories</option>'+cats.map(x=>`<option>${esc(x)}</option>`).join('');$('rheScope').innerHTML=`<b>${esc(META.system)}</b><br>${esc(META.scope)}<br><small>Bundled records intentionally avoid reproducing non-open proprietary descriptions. “Official index” means the option name/source is available, while the player enters mechanics from a lawfully owned source. Homebrew remains subject to DM/GM/MOL approval.</small>`;
['rheSearch','rheCategory','rheKind'].forEach(id=>$(id).addEventListener(id==='rheSearch'?'input':'change',renderCatalog));$('rheShowAll').onclick=e=>{e.currentTarget.dataset.all=e.currentTarget.dataset.all==='1'?'0':'1';e.currentTarget.textContent=e.currentTarget.dataset.all==='1'?'Limit Results':'Show All';renderCatalog()};
document.addEventListener('click',ev=>{const b=ev.target.closest('button');if(!b)return;if(b.dataset.rheAdd)addCatalog(b.dataset.rheAdd);if(b.dataset.rheRemove!==undefined){state.selected.splice(+b.dataset.rheRemove,1);save();renderSelected();renderLedger()}if(b.dataset.rheEffectAdd!==undefined){state.selected[+b.dataset.rheEffectAdd].effects.push({target:'',op:'add',value:'0',note:''});save();renderSelected()}if(b.dataset.rheEffectRemove){const [i,j]=b.dataset.rheEffectRemove.split(':').map(Number);state.selected[i].effects.splice(j,1);save();renderSelected();renderLedger()}if(b.dataset.rheBaseRemove!==undefined){state.bases.splice(+b.dataset.rheBaseRemove,1);save();renderBases();renderLedger()}if(b.dataset.rheFormRemove!==undefined){state.formulas.splice(+b.dataset.rheFormRemove,1);save();renderFormulas();renderLedger()}if(b.dataset.rheLinkRemove!==undefined){state.links.splice(+b.dataset.rheLinkRemove,1);save();renderLinks()}});
document.addEventListener('input',ev=>{const t=ev.target,i=+t.dataset.i;if(t.dataset.rheOpt){state.selected[i][t.dataset.rheOpt]=t.value;save();renderLedger()}if(t.dataset.rheEf){state.selected[i].effects[+t.dataset.j][t.dataset.rheEf]=t.value;save();renderLedger()}if(t.dataset.rheBase){state.bases[i][t.dataset.rheBase]=t.dataset.rheBase==='value'?Number(t.value):t.value;save();renderLedger()}if(t.dataset.rheForm){state.formulas[i][t.dataset.rheForm]=t.value;save();renderLedger()}if(t.dataset.rheLink){state.links[i][t.dataset.rheLink]=t.value;save()}});document.addEventListener('change',ev=>{const t=ev.target;if(t.dataset.rheActive!==undefined){state.selected[+t.dataset.rheActive].active=t.checked;save();renderLedger()}if(t.dataset.rheOpt||t.dataset.rheEf||t.dataset.rheLink)t.dispatchEvent(new Event('input',{bubbles:true}))});
$('rheAddManual').onclick=()=>{const name=$('rheNewName').value.trim(),category=$('rheNewCategory').value.trim();if(!name||!category){status('Manual options need both a category and name.','danger');return}state.selected.push({id:uid(),name,category,source:$('rheNewSource').value.trim(),approval:$('rheNewApproval').value,level:$('rheNewLevel').value.trim(),notes:$('rheNewNotes').value.trim(),active:true,effects:[]});$('rheNewName').value='';$('rheNewNotes').value='';save();renderSelected();renderLedger();status('Manual option added. Add its calculation effects below.','good')};
$('rheAddBase').onclick=()=>{state.bases.push({id:uid(),key:'',label:'',value:0});save();renderBases()};$('rheAddFormula').onclick=()=>{state.formulas.push({id:uid(),key:'',label:'',formula:'0'});save();renderFormulas()};$('rheAddLink').onclick=()=>{state.links.push({id:uid(),key:'',field:''});save();renderLinks()};$('rheApplyLinks').onclick=applyLinks;
$('rheExport').onclick=()=>download(`${SYSTEM_ID}-character-options-and-calculations.json`,state);$('rheCopy').onclick=async()=>{try{await navigator.clipboard.writeText(JSON.stringify(state,null,2));status('JSON copied.','good')}catch(e){status('Clipboard unavailable; use Export.','danger')}};$('rheImport').onchange=async ev=>{const f=ev.target.files[0];if(!f)return;try{const x=JSON.parse(await f.text());if(x.system&&x.system!==SYSTEM_ID&&!confirm(`This file was made for ${x.system}. Import anyway?`))return;state=Object.assign(state,x,{system:SYSTEM_ID});save();render();status('Imported successfully.','good')}catch(e){status('Import failed: '+e.message,'danger')}finally{ev.target.value=''}};$('rheReset').onclick=()=>{if(!confirm('Reset the options/homebrew engine for this sheet?'))return;localStorage.removeItem(STORE);state={version:2,system:SYSTEM_ID,selected:[],bases:[{id:uid(),key:'level',label:'Level / Rank / Tier',value:1}],formulas:[],links:[]};render();status('Engine reset.','good')};}

window.RulesHomebrewEngineAPI={
 system:SYSTEM_ID,
 meta:META,
 getCatalog:()=>CATALOG.map(x=>({id:x.id,name:x.name,category:x.category,source:x.source,kind:x.kind,summary:x.summary||''})),
 addById:(id,approval)=>{const x=CATALOG.find(v=>v.id===id);if(!x)return null;const o={id:uid(),catalogId:x.id,name:x.name,category:x.category,source:x.source,kind:x.kind,approval:approval||((x.kind==='official'||x.kind==='open')?'Official/open option':'Pending DM/GM/MOL review'),level:'',notes:x.summary||'',active:true,effects:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(x.effects||[]))};state.selected.push(o);save();renderSelected();renderLedger();return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(o))},
 addCustom:(data)=>{data=data||{};const o={id:uid(),catalogId:'',name:String(data.name||'Advancement choice'),category:String(data.category||'Custom Advancement'),source:String(data.source||'Level/advancement wizard'),kind:'custom',approval:String(data.approval||'Pending DM/GM/MOL review'),level:String(data.level||''),notes:String(data.notes||''),active:data.active!==false,effects:Array.isArray(data.effects)?JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(data.effects)):[]};state.selected.push(o);save();renderSelected();renderLedger();return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(o))}
};
function init(){if(!$('rules-homebrew-engine'))return;load();bind();render()}
if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',init);else init();
})();
</script>

const q=(s,r=document)=>r.querySelector(s), qa=(s,r=document)=>Array.from(r.querySelectorAll(s));
const clone=x=>JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(x));
const num=(v,d=0)=>Number.isFinite(Number(v))?Number(v):d;
const clamp=(v,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,v));
const esc=s=>String(s??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const norm=s=>String(s??'').normalize('NFKD').replace(/[’‘]/g,"'").replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/gi,' ').trim().toLowerCase();
const title=s=>String(s??'').replace(/[_-]+/g,' ').replace(/\b\w/g,c=>c.toUpperCase()).trim();
const arr=v=>Array.isArray(v)?v:(v==null||v===''?[]:[v]);
const uniq=a=>[...new Set(a.filter(Boolean).map(x=>String(x).trim()).filter(Boolean))];
const text=v=>typeof v==='string'?v:(v==null?'':JSON.stringify(v));
function systemId(){try{let s=window.__SHEET_TEST_API__?.selfTest?.().system||'';s=norm(s);if(s.includes('dnd'))return'dnd';if(s.includes('pathfinder')||s.includes('pf2'))return'pathfinder';if(s.includes('call of cthulhu')||s==='coc')return'coc';if(s.includes('dagger'))return'daggerheart';if(s.includes('blade'))return'blades';if(s.includes('savage')||s.includes('swade'))return'swade';if(s.includes('gurps'))return'gurps';if(s.includes('pbta')||s.includes('powered'))return'pbta';if(s.includes('fate'))return'fate'}catch(_){}const f=norm(location.pathname);return f.includes('pathfinder')?'pathfinder':f.includes('dnd')?'dnd':f.includes('daggerheart')?'daggerheart':f.includes('cthulhu')?'coc':f.includes('gurps')?'gurps':f.includes('swade')?'swade':f.includes('blades')?'blades':f.includes('pbta')?'pbta':'fate'}

function setState(s){if(!api()?.setState)throw Error('The sheet state API is not ready.');api().setState(clone(s));try{localStorage.setItem('universal-character-import:last-report',JSON.stringify(lastReport))}catch(_){}}
function flatten(o,p='',out={}){if(o==null)return out;if(Array.isArray(o)){o.forEach((v,i)=>flatten(v,p?`${p}.${i}`:String(i),out));return out}if(typeof o==='object'){Object.entries(o).forEach(([k,v])=>flatten(v,p?`${p}.${k}`:k,out));return out}out[norm(p)]=o;return out}
function pick(flat,...keys){for(const k of keys){const nk=norm(k);if(flat[nk]!=null&&flat[nk]!=='')return flat[nk];for(const [p,v] of Object.entries(flat))if((p===nk||p.endsWith(' '+nk))&&v!=null&&v!=='')return v}return''}
function listFrom(v){if(Array.isArray(v))return v.map(x=>typeof x==='string'?x:(x?.name||x?.id||JSON.stringify(x)));return String(v||'').split(/\r?\n|\s*[;,|]\s*/).map(x=>x.trim()).filter(Boolean)}
function score(a,b){a=norm(a);b=norm(b);if(!a||!b)return 0;if(a===b)return 100;if(a.includes(b)||b.includes(a))return 76;const A=new Set(a.split(' ')),B=new Set(b.split(' '));let i=0;A.forEach(x=>B.has(x)&&i++);return 50*(2*i/(A.size+B.size||1))}
function catalog(sys,cat){const d=LEGAL[sys]||{}, n=norm(cat),out=[];Object.entries(d).forEach(([k,v])=>{if(norm(k)===n||norm(k).includes(n)||n.includes(norm(k)))out.push(...v)});const c=CURATED[sys]||{};Object.entries(c).forEach(([k,v])=>{if(norm(k)===n||norm(k).includes(n)||n.includes(norm(k)))out.push(...v)});return uniq(out)}
function bestLegal(sys,cat,wanted,fallback,report,reason=''){const options=catalog(sys,cat);if(!options.length){const val=wanted||fallback||'';if(val)report.review.push(`${cat}: “${val}” could not be fully catalog-validated; retained for DM/GM/MOL review.`);return val}let best='',bestScore=-1;for(const o of options){const s=score(wanted,o);if(s>bestScore){best=o;bestScore=s}}if(wanted&&bestScore>=74)return best;const chosen=fallback&&options.find(o=>norm(o)===norm(fallback))||best||options[0];if(wanted&&norm(wanted)!==norm(chosen))report.replacements.push({category:cat,from:wanted,to:chosen,reason:reason||'No exact legal target-system match.'});return chosen}
